    36	
    37	def _state_change(old: TicketState, new: TicketState, cause: str) -> EventSpec:
    38	    return EventSpec(EventKind.state_changed, {"from": old.value, "to": new.value, "cause": cause})
    39	
    40	
    41	def _accept_gating_proposal(
    42	    ticket: Ticket,
    43	    field: FieldName,
    44	    stored_body: str,
    45	    resolved_by: str,
    46	    edited: bool,
    47	    grant: GrantPair | None,
    48	    cause: str,
    49	    superseded_body: str | None = None,
    50	) -> Decision:
    51	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
    52	    new_slot = FieldSlot(value=stored_body, proposal=None, notes=slot.notes)
    53	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
    54	    new_state = machine.advance_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling)
    55	    events: list[EventSpec] = []
    56	    if superseded_body is not None:
    57	        events.append(
    58	            EventSpec(
    59	                EventKind.proposal_superseded,
    60	                {"field": field.value, "replaced_body": superseded_body},
    61	            )
    62	        )
    63	    events.append(
    64	        EventSpec(
    65	            EventKind.proposal_accepted,
    66	            {
    67	                "field": field.value,
    68	                "body": stored_body,
    69	                "resolved_by": resolved_by,
    70	                "edited": edited,
    71	            },
    72	        )
    73	    )
    74	    events.append(_state_change(ticket.state, new_state, cause))
    75	    new_ceiling: TicketState | None = None
    76	    new_at_cap: AtCap | None = None
    77	    if grant is not None:
    78	        ceiling = grant.next_ceiling
    79	        assert isinstance(ceiling, TicketState)  # resolve_grant always yields a concrete state
    80	        new_ceiling = ceiling
    81	        new_at_cap = grant.at_cap
    82	        events.append(
    83	            EventSpec(
    84	                EventKind.grant_changed,
    85	                {
    86	                    "ceiling": ceiling.value,
    87	                    "at_cap": grant.at_cap.value,
    88	                    "cause": CAUSE_ONWARD_GRANT,
    89	                },
    90	            )
    91	        )
    92	    return Decision(
    93	        events=tuple(events),
    94	        new_fields=new_fields,
    95	        new_state=new_state,
    96	        new_ceiling=new_ceiling,
    97	        new_at_cap=new_at_cap,
    98	    )
    99	
   100	
   101	def decide_file_proposal(
   102	    ticket: Ticket, field: FieldName, body: str, actor: str, now: int
   103	) -> Decision:
   104	    admission.validate_body(body, "proposal body")
   105	    admission.check_agent_proposal(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling, ticket.at_cap, field)
   106	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
   107	    superseded_body = slot.proposal.body if slot.proposal is not None else None
   108	    if machine.auto_accept_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling, field) is not None:
   109	        return _accept_gating_proposal(
   110	            ticket,
   111	            field,
   112	            stored_body=body,
   113	            resolved_by=RESOLVED_BY_AUTO,
   114	            edited=False,
   115	            grant=None,
   116	            cause=CAUSE_AUTO_ACCEPT,
   117	            superseded_body=superseded_body,
   118	        )
   119	    new_slot = FieldSlot(
   120	        value=slot.value,
   121	        proposal=Proposal(body=body, proposed_by=actor, created_at=now),
   122	        notes=slot.notes,
   123	    )
   124	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
   125	    events: list[EventSpec] = []
   126	    if superseded_body is not None:
   127	        events.append(
   128	            EventSpec(
   129	                EventKind.proposal_superseded,
   130	                {"field": field.value, "replaced_body": superseded_body},
   131	            )
   132	        )
   133	    events.append(
   134	        EventSpec(
   135	            EventKind.proposal_filed,
   136	            {"field": field.value, "body": body, "proposed_by": actor},
   137	        )
   138	    )
   139	    return Decision(events=tuple(events), new_fields=new_fields)
   140	
   141	
   142	def decide_accept(
   143	    ticket: Ticket,
   144	    field: FieldName,
   145	    actor: str,
   146	    edited_body: str | None,
   147	    next_ceiling: NextCeiling | None,
   148	    at_cap: AtCap | None,
   149	) -> Decision:
   150	    admission.require_human(actor, "accept_proposal")
   151	    if edited_body is not None:
   152	        admission.validate_body(edited_body, "edit-accept text")
   153	    edited = edited_body is not None
   154	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
   155	    if slot.proposal is None:
   156	        raise PlannerError(
   157	            ErrorCode.not_found,
   158	            "no pending proposal on field",
   159	            {"ticket_id": ticket.id, "field": field.value},
   160	        )
   161	    stored_body = edited_body if edited_body is not None else slot.proposal.body
   162	    if field is machine.gating_field(ticket.state):
   163	        new_state = machine.advance_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling)
   164	        grant = machine.resolve_grant(new_state, next_ceiling, at_cap)
   165	        return _accept_gating_proposal(
   166	            ticket,
   167	            field,
   168	            stored_body=stored_body,
   169	            resolved_by=RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN,
   170	            edited=edited,
   171	            grant=grant,
   172	            cause=CAUSE_HUMAN_ACCEPT,
   173	        )
   174	    new_slot = FieldSlot(value=stored_body, proposal=None, notes=slot.notes)
   175	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
   176	    events = (
   177	        EventSpec(
   178	            EventKind.proposal_accepted,
   179	            {
   180	                "field": field.value,
   181	                "body": stored_body,
   182	                "resolved_by": RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN,
   183	                "edited": edited,
   184	            },
   185	        ),
   186	    )
   187	    return Decision(events=events, new_fields=new_fields)
   188	
   189	
   190	def decide_approve(ticket: Ticket, actor: str) -> Decision:
   191	    admission.require_human(actor, "approve_review")
   192	    if ticket.state is not TicketState.needs_review:
   193	        raise PlannerError(
   194	            ErrorCode.validation,
   195	            "approve requires state needs_review",
   196	            {"state": ticket.state.value},
   197	        )
   198	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, TicketState.done, CAUSE_REVIEW_APPROVE),)
   199	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=TicketState.done)
   200	
   201	
   202	def decide_state_jump(ticket: Ticket, new_state: TicketState, actor: str) -> Decision:
   203	    admission.require_human(actor, "set_state")
   204	    if new_state is TicketState.dropped:
   205	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "use the drop action")
   206	    if ticket.state is TicketState.dropped:
   207	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "dropped is terminal")
   208	    if new_state is ticket.state:
   209	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "ticket already in that state")
   210	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, new_state, CAUSE_HUMAN_STATE_JUMP),)
   211	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=new_state)
   212	
   213	
   214	def decide_drop(ticket: Ticket, actor: str) -> Decision:
   215	    admission.require_human(actor, "drop_ticket")
   216	    if ticket.state is TicketState.done:
   217	        raise PlannerError(
   218	            ErrorCode.validation, "done tickets cannot be dropped", {"state": "done"}
   219	        )
   220	    if ticket.state is TicketState.dropped:
   221	        raise PlannerError(
   222	            ErrorCode.validation, "ticket is already dropped", {"state": "dropped"}
   223	        )
   224	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, TicketState.dropped, CAUSE_DROP),)
   225	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=TicketState.dropped)
   226	
   227	
   228	def decide_grant_change(
   229	    ticket: Ticket, ceiling: TicketState, at_cap: AtCap, actor: str
   230	) -> Decision:
   231	    admission.require_human(actor, "change_grant")
   232	    machine.validate_ceiling(ceiling)
   233	    events = (
   234	        EventSpec(
   235	            EventKind.grant_changed,
   236	            {"ceiling": ceiling.value, "at_cap": at_cap.value, "cause": CAUSE_HUMAN_GRANT},
   237	        ),
   238	    )
   239	    return Decision(events=events, new_ceiling=ceiling, new_at_cap=at_cap)

     1	"""State-machine math for §4.2/§4.3/§4.4: linear order indexing, gating and
     2	advance tables, the auto-accept condition, ceiling comparison, and onward-grant
     3	resolution. Pure: contracts/errors + fields_codec only."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
     8	from planner.tickets.contracts import (
     9	    ADVANCE_TARGET,
    10	    GATING_FIELD,
    11	    NO_FURTHER,
    12	    STATE_ORDER,
    13	    AtCap,
    14	    FieldName,
    15	    GrantPair,
    16	    NextCeiling,
    17	    TicketFields,
    18	    TicketState,
    19	)
    20	from planner.tickets.logic import fields_codec
    21	
    22	
    23	def state_index(state: TicketState) -> int:
    24	    try:
    25	        return STATE_ORDER.index(state)
    26	    except ValueError as exc:
    27	        raise PlannerError(
    28	            ErrorCode.validation, "state outside the linear order", {"state": state.value}
    29	        ) from exc
    30	
    31	
    32	def is_terminal(state: TicketState) -> bool:
    33	    return state in (TicketState.done, TicketState.dropped)
    34	
    35	
    36	def gating_field(state: TicketState) -> FieldName | None:
    37	    return GATING_FIELD.get(state)
    38	
    39	
    40	def advance_target(state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState) -> TicketState:
    41	    if state is TicketState.in_progress and ceiling is TicketState.done:
    42	        return TicketState.done
    43	    target = ADVANCE_TARGET.get(state)
    44	    if target is None:
    45	        raise PlannerError(
    46	            ErrorCode.validation, "state has no advance target", {"state": state.value}
    47	        )
    48	    return target
    49	
    50	
    51	def auto_accept_target(
    52	    state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState, field: FieldName
    53	) -> TicketState | None:
    54	    if is_terminal(state):
    55	        return None
    56	    if field is not gating_field(state):
    57	        return None
    58	    target = advance_target(state, ceiling)
    59	    if state_index(target) > state_index(ceiling):
    60	        return None
    61	    return target
    62	
    63	
    64	def at_or_beyond_ceiling(state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState) -> bool:
    65	    return state_index(state) >= state_index(ceiling)
    66	
    67	
    68	def validate_ceiling(ceiling: TicketState) -> None:
    69	    if ceiling not in STATE_ORDER:
    70	        raise PlannerError(
    71	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "ceiling must be a linear state", {"ceiling": ceiling.value}
    72	        )
    73	
    74	
    75	def resolve_grant(
    76	    new_state: TicketState, next_ceiling: NextCeiling | None, at_cap: AtCap | None
    77	) -> GrantPair:
    78	    if next_ceiling is None or at_cap is None:
    79	        missing: list[str] = []
    80	        if next_ceiling is None:
    81	            missing.append("next_ceiling")
    82	        if at_cap is None:
    83	            missing.append("at_cap")
    84	        raise PlannerError(
    85	            ErrorCode.grant_missing, "accept requires the onward grant pair", {"missing": missing}
    86	        )
    87	    if next_ceiling == NO_FURTHER:
    88	        # the ceiling becomes exactly the newly entered state
    89	        return GrantPair(next_ceiling=new_state, at_cap=at_cap)
    90	    if not isinstance(next_ceiling, TicketState):
    91	        raise PlannerError(
    92	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown next_ceiling", {"next_ceiling": str(next_ceiling)}
    93	        )
    94	    if next_ceiling not in STATE_ORDER or state_index(next_ceiling) < state_index(new_state):
    95	        raise PlannerError(
    96	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid,
    97	            "next_ceiling must be at or beyond the new state",
    98	            {"next_ceiling": next_ceiling.value, "new_state": new_state.value},
    99	        )
   100	    return GrantPair(next_ceiling=next_ceiling, at_cap=at_cap)
   101	
   102	
   103	def has_pending_gating_proposal(state: TicketState, fields: TicketFields) -> bool:
   104	    field = gating_field(state)
   105	    if field is None:
   106	        return False
   107	    return fields_codec.get_slot(fields, field).proposal is not None

    38	def ticket_json(ticket: Ticket, now: int) -> JsonDict:
    39	    """§3.3 ticket. claim_lock is deliberately omitted (never echo the stored token);
    40	    the derived claim_active carries lease liveness instead."""

    42	        "id": ticket.id,

    60	        "created_at": ticket.created_at,

    90	
    91	def list_tickets(
    92	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    93	    now: int,
    94	    *,
    95	    state: TicketState | None,
    96	    project: Project | None,
    97	    sprint_id: str | None,
    98	    sprint_item_id: str | None,
    99	) -> list[JsonDict]:
   100	    clauses: list[str] = []
   101	    params: list[str] = []
   102	    if state is not None:
   103	        clauses.append("state = ?")
   104	        params.append(state.value)
   105	    if project is not None:
   106	        clauses.append("project = ?")
   107	        params.append(project.value)
   108	    if sprint_id is not None:
   109	        clauses.append("sprint_id = ?")
   110	        params.append(sprint_id)
   111	    if sprint_item_id is not None:
   112	        clauses.append("sprint_item_id = ?")
   113	        params.append(sprint_item_id)
   114	    where = (" WHERE " + " AND ".join(clauses)) if clauses else ""
   115	    rows = conn.execute(
   116	        "SELECT id FROM tickets" + where + " ORDER BY created_at ASC, id", tuple(params)
   117	    ).fetchall()
   118	    return [ticket_json(tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, str(row["id"])), now) for row in rows]
   119	
   120	
   121	def ticket_detail(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str, now: int) -> JsonDict:
   122	    ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   123	    detail = ticket_json(ticket, now)
   124	    link_rows = conn.execute(
   125	        "SELECT from_id, to_id, kind FROM links WHERE from_id = ? OR to_id = ? "
   126	        "ORDER BY kind, from_id, to_id",
   127	        (ticket_id, ticket_id),
   128	    ).fetchall()
   129	    day_rows = conn.execute(
   130	        "SELECT day_id FROM day_tickets WHERE ticket_id = ? ORDER BY day_id ASC", (ticket_id,)
   131	    ).fetchall()
   132	    total = int(
   133	        conn.execute(
   134	            "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ?", (ticket_id,)
   135	        ).fetchone()["n"]
   136	    )
   137	    running = conn.execute(
   138	        "SELECT 1 FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ? AND status = 'running' LIMIT 1", (ticket_id,)
   139	    ).fetchone()
   140	    latest = conn.execute(
   141	        "SELECT * FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ? ORDER BY started_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1",
   142	        (ticket_id,),
   143	    ).fetchone()
   144	    detail.update(
   145	        {
   146	            "blocked": core_links.is_blocked(conn, ticket_id),
   147	            "effective_sprint_id": tickets_data.get_effective_sprint_id(conn, ticket_id),
   148	            "links": [
   149	                {"from_id": str(r["from_id"]), "to_id": str(r["to_id"]), "kind": str(r["kind"])}
   150	                for r in link_rows
   151	            ],
   152	            "day_ids": [str(r["day_id"]) for r in day_rows],
   153	            "run_summary": {
   154	                "total": total,
   155	                "running": running is not None,
   156	                "latest": run_json(latest) if latest is not None else None,
   157	            },
   158	        }
   159	    )
   160	    return detail
   161	
   162	
   163	def runs_for_ticket(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str) -> list[JsonDict]:
   164	    rows = conn.execute(
   165	        "SELECT * FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ? ORDER BY started_at DESC, id DESC", (ticket_id,)
   166	    ).fetchall()
   167	    return [run_json(r) for r in rows]
   168	
   169	
   170	def get_run(conn: sqlite3.Connection, run_id: str) -> JsonDict | None:
   171	    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM runs WHERE id = ?", (run_id,)).fetchone()
   172	    return run_json(row) if row is not None else None
   173	
   174	
   175	def list_events_for_entity(conn: sqlite3.Connection, entity_id: str, limit: int) -> list[JsonDict]:
   176	    rows = conn.execute(
   177	        "SELECT id, entity_id, kind, payload, created_at FROM events WHERE entity_id = ? "
   178	        "ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT ?",
   179	        (entity_id, limit),
   180	    ).fetchall()
   181	    return [event_json(r) for r in rows]
   182	
   183	
   184	def copy_text(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str) -> str:
   185	    ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   186	    fields = ticket.fields
   187	
   188	    def show(value: str | None) -> str:
   189	        return value if value else "(none)"
   190	
   191	    link_rows = conn.execute(
   192	        "SELECT from_id, to_id, kind FROM links WHERE from_id = ? OR to_id = ? "
   193	        "ORDER BY kind, from_id, to_id",
   194	        (ticket_id, ticket_id),
   195	    ).fetchall()
   196	    if link_rows:
   197	        links_block = "\n".join(
   198	            f"- {str(r['kind'])}: {str(r['from_id'])} -> {str(r['to_id'])}" for r in link_rows
   199	        )
   200	    else:
   201	        links_block = "(none)"
   202	    return (
   203	        f"{ticket.title}\n"
   204	        f"state: {ticket.state.value}\n"
   205	        f"priority: {ticket.priority.value}\n"
   206	        f"\n"
   207	        f"success:\n{show(fields.success.value)}\n"
   208	        f"\n"
   209	        f"approach:\n{show(fields.approach.value)}\n"
   210	        f"\n"
   211	        f"plan:\n{show(fields.plan.value)}\n"
   212	        f"\n"
   213	        f"result:\n{show(fields.result.value)}\n"
   214	        f"\n"
   215	        f"recap:\n{show(ticket.recap)}\n"
   216	        f"\n"
   217	        f"links:\n{links_block}\n"
   218	    )
   219	
   220	
   221	# --- board (§10.3) -------------------------------------------------------------
   222	
   223	
   224	def board_view(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int) -> JsonDict:
   225	    rows = conn.execute(
   226	        "SELECT id, title, state, priority, deadline, project, fields, claim_lock, "
   227	        "claim_expires, created_at FROM tickets WHERE state != 'dropped'"
   228	    ).fetchall()
   229	    by_state: dict[str, list[tuple[tuple[int, int, str, int], JsonDict]]] = {
   230	        s.value: [] for s in STATE_ORDER
   231	    }
   232	    for row in rows:
   233	        state = str(row["state"])
   234	        priority = str(row["priority"])
   235	        deadline = str(row["deadline"]) if row["deadline"] is not None else None
   236	        fields = json.loads(str(row["fields"]))
   237	        claim_lock = str(row["claim_lock"]) if row["claim_lock"] is not None else None
   238	        claim_expires = int(row["claim_expires"]) if row["claim_expires"] is not None else None
   239	        card: JsonDict = {
   240	            "id": str(row["id"]),
   241	            "title": str(row["title"]),
   242	            "priority": priority,
   243	            "deadline": deadline,
   244	            "project": str(row["project"]) if row["project"] is not None else None,
   245	            "has_pending_proposal": gating_field_pending(TicketState(state), fields),
   246	            "has_running_claim": has_active_claim(claim_lock, claim_expires, now),
   247	        }
   248	        sort_key = (
   249	            _prio_rank(priority),
   250	            0 if deadline is not None else 1,
   251	            deadline or "",
   252	            int(row["created_at"]),
   253	        )
   254	        by_state[state].append((sort_key, card))
   255	    columns: list[JsonDict] = []
   256	    for s in STATE_ORDER:
   257	        cards = [card for _, card in sorted(by_state[s.value], key=lambda item: item[0])]
   258	        columns.append({"state": s.value, "cards": cards})
   259	    return {"columns": columns}
   260	
   261	
   262	# --- queues (§4.5) -------------------------------------------------------------
   263	
   264	
   265	def _entity_type(entity_id: str) -> str:
   266	    return "ticket" if entity_id.split("_", 1)[0] == "t" else "item"
   267	
   268	
   269	def _approvals(conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_approval_rows: list[JsonDict]) -> list[JsonDict]:
   270	    ticket_rows = conn.execute(
   271	        "SELECT id, title, state, fields, updated_at FROM tickets "
   272	        "WHERE state NOT IN ('done','dropped') ORDER BY id"
   273	    ).fetchall()
   274	    ticket_digest: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   275	    ticket_title: dict[str, str] = {}
   276	    ticket_updated: dict[str, int] = {}
   277	    for r in ticket_rows:
   278	        tid = str(r["id"])
   279	        ticket_digest.append(
   280	            {
   281	                "id": tid,
   282	                "state": str(r["state"]),
   283	                "fields": json.loads(str(r["fields"])),
   284	                "updated_at": int(r["updated_at"]),
   285	            }
   286	        )
   287	        ticket_title[tid] = str(r["title"])
   288	        ticket_updated[tid] = int(r["updated_at"])
   289	    item_digest: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   290	    item_title: dict[str, str] = {}
   291	    for r in item_approval_rows:
   292	        iid = str(r["id"])
   293	        item_digest.append({"id": iid, "status_proposal": r["status_proposal"]})
   294	        item_title[iid] = str(r["title"])
   295	    digest = approvals_digest(ticket_digest, item_digest)
   296	    # A5: review entries use the last state_changed->needs_review event time, not the
   297	    # updated_at proxy; the boundary digest keeps its documented proxy.
   298	    for entry in digest:
   299	        if entry["kind"] == "review":
   300	            tid = str(entry["entity_id"])
   301	            row = conn.execute(
   302	                "SELECT created_at FROM events WHERE entity_id = ? AND kind = 'state_changed' "
   303	                "AND json_extract(payload, '$.to') = 'needs_review' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1",
   304	                (tid,),
   305	            ).fetchone()
   306	            entry["waiting_since"] = (
   307	                int(row["created_at"]) if row is not None
   308	                else ticket_updated.get(tid, entry["waiting_since"])
   309	            )
   310	    digest.sort(key=lambda e: e["waiting_since"])
   311	    result: list[JsonDict] = []
   312	    for entry in digest:
   313	        eid = str(entry["entity_id"])
   314	        etype = _entity_type(eid)
   315	        title = ticket_title.get(eid) if etype == "ticket" else item_title.get(eid)
   316	        result.append(
   317	            {
   318	                "entity_id": eid,
   319	                "entity_type": etype,
   320	                "kind": entry["kind"],
   321	                "title": title,
   322	                "waiting_since": entry["waiting_since"],
   323	            }
   324	        )
   325	    return result
   326	
   327	
   328	def _pickup(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int) -> list[JsonDict]:
   329	    candidates = dispatch_data.load_candidates(conn, now)
   330	    eligible = [c for c in candidates if is_eligible(c)]
   331	    eligible.sort(key=ordering_key)
   332	    if not eligible:
   333	        return []
   334	    ids = [c.ticket_id for c in eligible]
   335	    placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in ids)
   336	    rows = conn.execute(
   337	        f"SELECT id, title FROM tickets WHERE id IN ({placeholders})", tuple(ids)
   338	    ).fetchall()
   339	    title_by_id = {str(r["id"]): str(r["title"]) for r in rows}
   340	    return [
   341	        {
   342	            "ticket_id": c.ticket_id,
   343	            "title": title_by_id.get(c.ticket_id, ""),
   344	            "state": c.state.value,
   345	            "priority": c.priority.value,
   346	            "deadline": c.deadline,
   347	        }
   348	        for c in eligible
   349	    ]
   350	
   351	
   352	def _overdue(
   353	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, today_iso: str, item_overdue_rows: list[JsonDict]
   354	) -> list[JsonDict]:
   355	    ticket_rows = conn.execute(
   356	        "SELECT id, title, state, priority, deadline FROM tickets WHERE deadline IS NOT NULL "
   357	        "ORDER BY deadline ASC, id"
   358	    ).fetchall()
   359	    ticket_dicts: list[dict[str, object]] = [
   360	        {
   361	            "id": str(r["id"]),
   362	            "title": str(r["title"]),
   363	            "state": str(r["state"]),
   364	            "priority": str(r["priority"]),
   365	            "deadline": str(r["deadline"]) if r["deadline"] is not None else None,
   366	        }
   367	        for r in ticket_rows
   368	    ]
   369	    item_dicts: list[dict[str, object]] = [dict(r) for r in item_overdue_rows]
   370	    digest = overdue_list(ticket_dicts, item_dicts, today_iso)
   371	    ticket_deadline = {str(d["id"]): d["deadline"] for d in ticket_dicts}
   372	    item_deadline = {str(d["id"]): d["deadline"] for d in item_dicts}
   373	    result: list[JsonDict] = []
   374	    for entry in digest:
   375	        eid = str(entry["id"])
   376	        etype = _entity_type(eid)
   377	        deadline = ticket_deadline.get(eid) if etype == "ticket" else item_deadline.get(eid)
   378	        result.append(
   379	            {
   380	                "id": eid,
   381	                "entity_type": etype,
   382	                "title": entry["title"],
   383	                "state": entry["state"],
   384	                "priority": entry["priority"],
   385	                "deadline": deadline,
   386	            }
   387	        )
   388	    return result
   389	
   390	
   391	def queues_view(
   392	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   393	    now: int,
   394	    today_iso: str,
   395	    item_approval_rows: list[JsonDict],
   396	    item_overdue_rows: list[JsonDict],
   397	) -> JsonDict:
   398	    return {
   399	        "approvals": _approvals(conn, item_approval_rows),
   400	        "pickup": _pickup(conn, now),
   401	        "overdue": _overdue(conn, today_iso, item_overdue_rows),
   402	    }

     1	"""§7.6 request context and write validation. This is the server-side dependency
     2	that T10/T13 route bodies consume: it reads the X-Plan-* headers, classifies each
     3	request as a claimed agent, a plain agent, or the human, and validates a presented
     4	claim against a ticket's active lease. Lease liveness is decided only by
     5	dispatch.logic.claims — never re-derived here.
     6	
     7	FastAPI appears here because this is part of the server shell (the server.py
     8	family), not a logic or data module."""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import sqlite3
    13	from dataclasses import dataclass
    14	from typing import Final, NoReturn
    15	
    16	from fastapi import Request
    17	
    18	from planner.core.contracts import JsonDict
    19	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    20	from planner.dispatch.logic.claims import has_active_claim, is_expired
    21	
    22	X_PLAN_RUN_ID: Final = "X-Plan-Run-Id"
    23	X_PLAN_CLAIM: Final = "X-Plan-Claim"
    24	X_PLAN_ACTOR: Final = "X-Plan-Actor"
    25	_DEFAULT_AGENT_ACTOR: Final = "agent"    # §7.6: PLAN_ACTOR default "agent"
    26	_HUMAN_ACTOR: Final = "human"
    27	
    28	
    29	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    30	class RequestContext:
    31	    actor: str
    32	    run_id: str | None
    33	    claim: str | None
    34	    is_claimed_agent: bool
    35	    is_human: bool
    36	
    37	
    38	def _normalize(raw: str | None) -> str | None:
    39	    """strip(); an empty or whitespace-only header is an absent header."""
    40	    if raw is None:
    41	        return None
    42	    stripped = raw.strip()
    43	    return stripped or None
    44	
    45	
    46	def _classify(run_id: str | None, claim: str | None, actor: str | None) -> RequestContext:
    47	    """Pure classification over the §1.1 truth table; inputs are pre-normalized."""
    48	    if run_id is not None or claim is not None:
    49	        # Any claim header present (whole or half) is a claimed-agent attempt.
    50	        return RequestContext(
    51	            actor=actor if actor is not None else _DEFAULT_AGENT_ACTOR,
    52	            run_id=run_id,
    53	            claim=claim,
    54	            is_claimed_agent=True,
    55	            is_human=False,
    56	        )
    57	    if actor is not None:
    58	        # Non-dispatched agent context (planner-main chat agent): proposer from PLAN_ACTOR.
    59	        return RequestContext(
    60	            actor=actor,
    61	            run_id=None,
    62	            claim=None,
    63	            is_claimed_agent=False,
    64	            is_human=False,
    65	        )
    66	    # The human: UI requests carry no agent headers.
    67	    return RequestContext(
    68	        actor=_HUMAN_ACTOR,
    69	        run_id=None,
    70	        claim=None,
    71	        is_claimed_agent=False,
    72	        is_human=True,
    73	    )
    74	
    75	
    76	def request_context(request: Request) -> RequestContext:
    77	    """FastAPI dependency: reads the three headers, normalizes, classifies. No DB, no clock."""
    78	    headers = request.headers
    79	    return _classify(
    80	        _normalize(headers.get(X_PLAN_RUN_ID)),
    81	        _normalize(headers.get(X_PLAN_CLAIM)),
    82	        _normalize(headers.get(X_PLAN_ACTOR)),
    83	    )
    84	
    85	
    86	def _reject_stale(
    87	    reason: str,
    88	    message: str,
    89	    ctx: RequestContext,
    90	    ticket_id: str,
    91	    active_claim_present: bool,
    92	    stored_lock: str | None,
    93	    extra: JsonDict | None = None,
    94	) -> NoReturn:
    95	    # Presented values are echoed back — except a presented token equal to the stored
    96	    # claim_lock (the expired/run_mismatch/missing-half paths reach here with a correct
    97	    # token): verbatim it would put the live stored token into a detail that may be
    98	    # logged or displayed, so it is redacted.
    99	    presented_claim = ctx.claim
   100	    if presented_claim is not None and presented_claim == stored_lock:
   101	        presented_claim = "(redacted)"
   102	    detail: JsonDict = {
   103	        "reason": reason,
   104	        "ticket_id": ticket_id,
   105	        "presented_run_id": ctx.run_id,
   106	        "presented_claim": presented_claim,
   107	        "active_claim_present": active_claim_present,
   108	    }
   109	    if extra is not None:
   110	        detail.update(extra)
   111	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.stale_claim, message, detail)
   112	
   113	
   114	def require_claim(
   115	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, ctx: RequestContext, ticket_id: str, now: int
   116	) -> None:
   117	    """§7.6 write validation. Checks run in the fixed order (ticket exists → active
   118	    claim → unexpired → token → run id); the first failure raises stale_claim naming
   119	    which check failed. The stored token is never echoed. now is unix seconds."""
   120	    row = conn.execute(
   121	        "SELECT claim_lock, claim_expires FROM tickets WHERE id=?", (ticket_id,)
   122	    ).fetchone()
   123	    if row is None:
   124	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "ticket not found", {"ticket_id": ticket_id})
   125	    claim_lock = str(row["claim_lock"]) if row["claim_lock"] is not None else None
   126	    claim_expires = int(row["claim_expires"]) if row["claim_expires"] is not None else None
   127	    active = has_active_claim(claim_lock, claim_expires, now)
   128	
   129	    if ctx.run_id is None or ctx.claim is None:
   130	        missing = [
   131	            name
   132	            for name, value in ((X_PLAN_RUN_ID, ctx.run_id), (X_PLAN_CLAIM, ctx.claim))
   133	            if value is None
   134	        ]
   135	        _reject_stale(
   136	            "missing_header",
   137	            "claim headers incomplete",
   138	            ctx,
   139	            ticket_id,
   140	            active,
   141	            claim_lock,
   142	            {"missing": missing},
   143	        )
   144	    if claim_lock is None:
   145	        _reject_stale(
   146	            "none_active", "ticket has no active claim", ctx, ticket_id, active, claim_lock
   147	        )
   148	    if is_expired(claim_expires, now):
   149	        _reject_stale(
   150	            "expired",
   151	            "claim expired",
   152	            ctx,
   153	            ticket_id,
   154	            active,
   155	            claim_lock,
   156	            {"claim_expires": claim_expires},
   157	        )
   158	    if ctx.claim != claim_lock:
   159	        _reject_stale(
   160	            "foreign",
   161	            "claim token does not match the ticket's active claim",
   162	            ctx,
   163	            ticket_id,
   164	            active,
   165	            claim_lock,
   166	        )
   167	    run_row = conn.execute(
   168	        "SELECT id FROM runs WHERE ticket_id=? AND status='running' "
   169	        "ORDER BY started_at DESC LIMIT 1",
   170	        (ticket_id,),
   171	    ).fetchone()
   172	    if run_row is None or str(run_row["id"]) != ctx.run_id:
   173	        _reject_stale(
   174	            "run_mismatch",
   175	            "run id does not match the ticket's running run",
   176	            ctx,
   177	            ticket_id,
   178	            active,
   179	            claim_lock,
   180	        )
   181	    return None
   182	
   183	
   184	def reject_agents(ctx: RequestContext) -> None:
   185	    """(H) routes: any agent-classified request (claimed or plain) is agent_forbidden."""
   186	    if ctx.is_human:
   187	        return None
   188	    raise PlannerError(
   189	        ErrorCode.agent_forbidden,
   190	        "human-only action",
   191	        {"actor": ctx.actor, "is_claimed_agent": ctx.is_claimed_agent},
   192	    )

     1	"""Run routes (§9): heartbeat and close. Both require the claim headers — a request
     2	with no claim headers fails inside require_claim with stale_claim/missing_header.
     3	require_claim takes a ticket id, so each route resolves run -> ticket_id first via the
     4	run reader homed in tickets/views. close restricts the outcome to the agent-reportable

     7	re-implementation of close_run's own check."""

    30	@router.post("/runs/{run_id}/heartbeat")
    31	async def heartbeat(run_id: str, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
    32	    now = clk.now_unix()
    33	    run = tickets_views.get_run(conn, run_id)
    34	    if run is None:
    35	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "run not found", {"run_id": run_id})
    36	    require_claim(conn, ctx, run["ticket_id"], now)

    39	    return {"run": tickets_views.get_run(conn, run_id), "claim_expires": new_expires}

    42	@router.post("/runs/{run_id}/close")

    44	                    clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
    45	    now = clk.now_unix()
    46	    run = tickets_views.get_run(conn, run_id)
    47	    if run is None:
    48	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "run not found", {"run_id": run_id})
    49	    require_claim(conn, ctx, run["ticket_id"], now)

    53	            ErrorCode.validation, "outcome must be done or blocked", {"outcome": body.outcome}
    54	        )
    55	    with txn(conn):

    57	    return {"run": tickets_views.get_run(conn, run_id)}

     1	"""The CLI's single HTTP seam. Owns URL construction, header assembly, request
     2	execution, and — the whole point — every stdout/stderr/exit-code decision. A
     3	handler in main.py only builds a route + body, calls `send`, then `emit` (or a
     4	client-side `fail_validation`). This module imports stdlib + httpx only; error
     5	envelopes are plain dict literals, never a planner type."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	import json
    10	import os
    11	import sys
    12	from typing import Any, NoReturn
    13	
    14	import httpx
    15	
    16	_DEFAULT_BASE_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8767"
    17	_TIMEOUT = 30.0
    18	
    19	EXIT_OK = 0
    20	EXIT_ERROR = 1
    21	EXIT_CONNECTION = 2
    22	
    23	
    24	def _base_url() -> str:
    25	    raw = os.environ.get("PLAN_SERVER_URL", "").strip()
    26	    return raw or _DEFAULT_BASE_URL
    27	
    28	
    29	def _url(path: str) -> str:
    30	    return _base_url().rstrip("/") + path
    31	
    32	
    33	def _headers() -> dict[str, str]:
    34	    # X-Plan-Actor is always sent (default "agent"); run/claim only when set & non-empty.
    35	    headers = {"X-Plan-Actor": os.environ.get("PLAN_ACTOR", "").strip() or "agent"}
    36	    run_id = os.environ.get("PLAN_RUN_ID", "").strip()
    37	    if run_id:
    38	        headers["X-Plan-Run-Id"] = run_id
    39	    claim = os.environ.get("PLAN_CLAIM", "").strip()
    40	    if claim:
    41	        headers["X-Plan-Claim"] = claim
    42	    return headers
    43	
    44	
    45	def send(
    46	    method: str,
    47	    path: str,
    48	    *,
    49	    as_json: bool,
    50	    json_body: Any | None = None,
    51	    params: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    52	) -> Any:
    53	    """Execute one request and apply the failure half of the exit contract. Transport
    54	    failure -> stderr + exit 2. Non-2xx (or a 2xx body that still carries an "error"
    55	    key) -> stderr + exit 1. On 2xx: return the parsed JSON body to the caller."""
    56	    try:
    57	        resp = httpx.request(
    58	            method, _url(path), json=json_body, params=params, headers=_headers(),
    59	            timeout=_TIMEOUT,
    60	        )
    61	    except httpx.TransportError as exc:
    62	        _fail_connection(exc, as_json)
    63	    try:
    64	        data: Any = resp.json()
    65	    except ValueError:
    66	        data = None
    67	    if resp.is_success:
    68	        if isinstance(data, dict) and "error" in data:
    69	            _fail_response(resp, data, as_json)  # A1: unconditional error-envelope contract
    70	        return data
    71	    _fail_response(resp, data, as_json)
    72	
    73	
    74	def emit(data: Any, as_json: bool, human: str) -> NoReturn:
    75	    """Success path. --json: raw JSON to stdout. Else: the terse human line. exit 0."""
    76	    if as_json:
    77	        print(json.dumps(data))
    78	    else:
    79	        print(human)
    80	    sys.exit(EXIT_OK)
    81	
    82	
    83	def fail_validation(
    84	    message: str, as_json: bool, detail: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    85	) -> NoReturn:
    86	    """Client-side validation error, rendered in the same style as a server error. exit 1."""
    87	    payload = {"error": {"code": "validation", "message": message, "detail": detail or {}}}
    88	    if as_json:
    89	        print(json.dumps(payload), file=sys.stderr)
    90	    else:
    91	        print(f"error: validation: {message}", file=sys.stderr)
    92	    sys.exit(EXIT_ERROR)
    93	
    94	
    95	def _fail_connection(exc: httpx.TransportError, as_json: bool) -> NoReturn:
    96	    payload = {"error": {"code": "connection", "message": str(exc), "detail": {}}}
    97	    if as_json:
    98	        print(json.dumps(payload), file=sys.stderr)
    99	    else:
   100	        print(f"error: connection: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
   101	    sys.exit(EXIT_CONNECTION)
   102	
   103	
   104	def _fail_response(resp: httpx.Response, data: Any, as_json: bool) -> NoReturn:
   105	    if isinstance(data, dict) and "error" in data:
   106	        payload: dict[str, Any] = data
   107	    else:
   108	        # Non-envelope non-2xx (422 pydantic, 404 routing, 500 HTML). Never swallow:
   109	        # wrap so exit is 1 and stderr still carries a parseable {"error":...}.
   110	        payload = {
   111	            "error": {
   112	                "code": "http_error",
   113	                "message": f"HTTP {resp.status_code}",
   114	                "detail": data if data is not None else resp.text,
   115	            }
   116	        }
   117	    if as_json:
   118	        print(json.dumps(payload), file=sys.stderr)
   119	    else:
   120	        err = payload["error"]
   121	        print(f"error: {err['code']}: {err['message']}", file=sys.stderr)
   122	    sys.exit(EXIT_ERROR)

     1	"""The dispatcher tick (§7.1). One synchronous tick: fail-safe dispatch_enabled
     2	re-read, machine-wide advisory flock, reclaim sweep, run-timeout enforcement,
     3	eligibility recompute, claim-then-spawn up to the concurrency cap. In test mode no
     4	code path ever signals a real pid (fake pids may collide with live processes). The
     5	lock is acquired once and held by the process; ``release_dispatcher_lock`` is the
     6	explicit release for loops.stop() and tests."""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import fcntl
    11	import os
    12	import signal
    13	import sqlite3
    14	from collections.abc import Callable
    15	from pathlib import Path
    16	from typing import Final

    28	ConnFactory = Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection]
    29	
    30	# One cached OS fd per lock path. The first successful acquisition caches the fd; the
    31	# process holds the flock from then on (released only on process exit or explicit
    32	# release).
    33	_LOCK_FDS: Final[dict[str, int]] = {}
    34	
    35	
    36	def _ensure_dispatcher_lock(lock_path: str) -> bool:
    37	    """Acquire (once) the machine-wide advisory lock; True when this process holds it."""
    38	    if lock_path in _LOCK_FDS:
    39	        return True
    40	    Path(lock_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    41	    fd = os.open(lock_path, os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT, 0o644)
    42	    try:
    43	        fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
    44	    except BlockingIOError:
    45	        os.close(fd)
    46	        return False
    47	    _LOCK_FDS[lock_path] = fd
    48	    return True
    49	
    50	
    51	def release_dispatcher_lock(lock_path: str) -> None:
    52	    """Explicit release used by loops.stop() and tests. Safe no-op when not held."""
    53	    fd = _LOCK_FDS.pop(lock_path, None)
    54	    if fd is not None:
    55	        fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
    56	        os.close(fd)
    57	
    58	
    59	def _pid_alive(pid: int) -> bool:
    60	    """Real-mode liveness probe via signal 0."""
    61	    try:
    62	        os.kill(pid, 0)
    63	    except ProcessLookupError:
    64	        return False
    65	    except PermissionError:
    66	        return True
    67	    return True
    68	
    69	
    70	def _pid_alive_always(_pid: int) -> bool:
    71	    """Test-mode liveness probe: never signals, always reports alive."""
    72	    return True
    73	
    74	
    75	def _sigterm(pid: int) -> None:
    76	    """Real-mode kill: SIGTERM, guarded against an already-gone pid."""
    77	    try:
    78	        os.kill(pid, signal.SIGTERM)
    79	    except ProcessLookupError:
    80	        pass
    81	
    82	
    83	def _kill_noop(_pid: int) -> None:
    84	    """Test-mode kill: never signals."""
    85	    return None
    86	
    87	
    88	def _enforce_run_timeouts(
    89	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    90	    now: int,
    91	    run_max_seconds: int,
    92	    failure_limit: int,
    93	    kill: Callable[[int], None],
    94	) -> list[str]:
    95	    """Close every running run that has exceeded ``run_max_seconds`` (fires at exactly
    96	    the boundary). A5: close FIRST (guarded by a PlannerError skip); only the run whose
    97	    close won the status CAS is then signalled and reported."""
    98	    rows = conn.execute(
    99	        "SELECT id, pid FROM runs WHERE status='running' AND started_at <= ?",
   100	        (now - run_max_seconds,),
   101	    ).fetchall()
   102	    closed: list[str] = []
   103	    for row in rows:
   104	        run_id = str(row["id"])
   105	        pid = int(row["pid"]) if row["pid"] is not None else None
   106	        try:
   107	            close_run(
   108	                conn,
   109	                run_id,
   110	                RunStatus.timed_out,
   111	                now,
   112	                failure_limit,
   113	                error=f"exceeded run_max_seconds ({run_max_seconds}s)",
   114	            )
   115	        except PlannerError:
   116	            continue
   117	        if pid is not None:
   118	            kill(pid)
   119	        closed.append(run_id)
   120	    return closed
   121	
   122	
   123	def run_tick(
   124	    conn_factory: ConnFactory, config: Config, clock: Clock, adapters: Adapters
   125	) -> JsonDict:
   126	    """One §7.1-ordered dispatcher tick. Returns the pinned five-key report."""
   127	    report: JsonDict = {
   128	        "skipped": None,
   129	        "reclaimed": [],
   130	        "timed_out": [],
   131	        "spawned": [],
   132	        "spawn_failed": [],
   133	    }
   134	
   135	    # (0) fail-safe dispatch flag — before the lock and before any connection.
   136	    if not read_dispatch_enabled():
   137	        report["skipped"] = "dispatch_disabled"
   138	        return report
   139	
   140	    # (0b) machine-wide advisory lock — a skipped tick opens no connection.
   141	    if not _ensure_dispatcher_lock(config.dispatcher_lock_path):
   142	        report["skipped"] = "lock_held"
   143	        return report
   144	
   145	    conn = conn_factory()
   146	    try:
   147	        now = clock.now_unix()
   148	
   149	        # (1) reclaim expired claims / dead pids.
   150	        pid_alive = _pid_alive_always if config.test_mode else _pid_alive
   151	        report["reclaimed"] = sweep_reclaims(conn, now, pid_alive, config.failure_limit)
   152	
   153	        # (2) enforce per-run max runtime.
   154	        kill = _kill_noop if config.test_mode else _sigterm
   155	        report["timed_out"] = _enforce_run_timeouts(
   156	            conn, now, config.run_max_seconds, config.failure_limit, kill
   157	        )
   158	
   159	        # (3) recompute eligibility, ordered.
   160	        candidates = load_candidates(conn, now)
   161	        eligible = sorted((c for c in candidates if is_eligible(c)), key=ordering_key)
   162	
   163	        # (4) claim-then-spawn up to the concurrency cap.
   164	        active = int(
   165	            conn.execute(
   166	                "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM runs WHERE status='running'"
   167	            ).fetchone()["n"]
   168	        )
   169	        budget = max(config.max_runs - active, 0)
   170	        Path(config.logs_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   171	        for cand in eligible:
   172	            if budget == 0:
   173	                break
   174	            budget -= 1  # every claim attempt consumes one unit, win or lose (RD-2).
   175	            claimed = claim(conn, cand.ticket_id, now, config.claim_ttl_seconds)
   176	            if claimed is None:
   177	                continue
   178	            run_id, token = claimed
   179	            request = SpawnRequest(
   180	                ticket_id=cand.ticket_id,
   181	                run_id=run_id,
   182	                claim=token,
   183	                server_url=f"http://{HOST}:{config.port}",
   184	                log_path=str(Path(config.logs_dir) / f"{run_id}.log"),
   185	                hermes_bin=config.hermes_bin,
   186	                profile=config.hermes_profile,
   187	                skill=config.worker_skill,
   188	            )
   189	            try:
   190	                result = adapters.spawn.spawn(request)
   191	            except Exception as exc:  # adapter raise = spawn failure (RD-9).
   192	                result = SpawnResult(ok=False, error=str(exc))
   193	            if result.ok:
   194	                conn.execute(
   195	                    "UPDATE runs SET pid=? WHERE id=?", (result.pid, run_id)
   196	                )
   197	                report["spawned"].append(
   198	                    {"ticket_id": cand.ticket_id, "run_id": run_id, "pid": result.pid}
   199	                )
   200	            else:
   201	                close_run(
   202	                    conn,
   203	                    run_id,
   204	                    RunStatus.spawn_failed,
   205	                    now,
   206	                    config.failure_limit,
   207	                    error=result.error or "spawn failed",
   208	                )
   209	                report["spawn_failed"].append(
   210	                    {
   211	                        "ticket_id": cand.ticket_id,
   212	                        "run_id": run_id,
   213	                        "error": result.error or "spawn failed",
   214	                    }
   215	                )
   216	    finally:
   217	        conn.close()
   218	    return report

     1	"""The dispatch data layer over sqlite3 — claims (§7.3), heartbeat, reclaim, run
     2	close + circuit breaker (§7.5), and candidate assembly (§7.2 inputs). Config values
     3	(ttl_seconds, failure_limit) are always parameters; this module never reads config or
     4	the clock — every function takes now: int.
     5	
     6	Single-winner guarantees come from two CAS statements: the claim CAS on
     7	tickets.claim_lock and the finalize CAS on runs.status, each one atomic UPDATE.
     8	Follow-up statements run only on the CAS winner's path, so they cannot double-run."""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import json
    13	import sqlite3
    14	from collections.abc import Callable
    15	from typing import Final, NamedTuple
    16	
    17	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind, JsonDict, Priority
    18	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    19	from planner.core.events import append_event
    20	from planner.core.ids import new_claim, new_id
    21	from planner.core.links import blocked_target_ids
    22	from planner.dispatch.contracts import (
    23	    AGENT_CLOSE_OUTCOMES,
    24	    FAILURE_STATUSES,
    25	    DispatchCandidate,
    26	    RunStatus,
    27	)
    28	from planner.dispatch.logic import (
    29	    expiry_at,
    30	    gating_field_pending,
    31	    has_active_claim,
    32	    is_expired,
    33	    next_breaker_state,
    34	)
    35	from planner.tickets.contracts import AtCap, TicketState

    37	# The statuses close_run accepts: agent outcomes plus failures (D20). running and
    38	# reclaimed are rejected — reclaimed is reachable only through sweep_reclaims.
    39	_CLOSABLE: Final[frozenset[RunStatus]] = AGENT_CLOSE_OUTCOMES | FAILURE_STATUSES
    40	
    41	
    42	def claim(
    43	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    44	    ticket_id: str,
    45	    now: int,
    46	    ttl_seconds: int,
    47	    pid: int | None = None,
    48	) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
    49	    """§7.3 claim: the CAS is the sole arbiter against double-claim. Returns
    50	    (run_id, claim_token) on the win, or None when lost (rowcount 0 — indistinguishable
    51	    from an unknown ticket id, D13). No eligibility re-check, no updated_at bump (D7)."""
    52	    token = new_claim()
    53	    expires = expiry_at(now, ttl_seconds)
    54	    cursor = conn.execute(
    55	        "UPDATE tickets SET claim_lock=?, claim_expires=? WHERE id=? AND claim_lock IS NULL",
    56	        (token, expires, ticket_id),
    57	    )
    58	    if cursor.rowcount == 0:
    59	        return None
    60	    run_id = new_id("run")
    61	    conn.execute(
    62	        "INSERT INTO runs (id, ticket_id, status, started_at, ended_at, summary, error, pid) "
    63	        "VALUES (?, ?, 'running', ?, NULL, NULL, NULL, ?)",
    64	        (run_id, ticket_id, now, pid),
    65	    )
    66	    append_event(conn, ticket_id, EventKind.run_started, {"run_id": run_id, "pid": pid}, now)
    67	    return run_id, token
    68	
    69	
    70	def heartbeat(conn: sqlite3.Connection, run_id: str, now: int, ttl_seconds: int) -> int:
    71	    """Extend the lease by exactly one TTL from now (D2), returning the new expiry.
    72	    A missing run is not_found; a non-running run or an expired/cleared claim is
    73	    stale_claim (D3) — an expired lease is never revived by a late heartbeat."""
    74	    row = conn.execute("SELECT ticket_id, status FROM runs WHERE id=?", (run_id,)).fetchone()
    75	    if row is None:
    76	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "run not found", {"run_id": run_id})
    77	    if str(row["status"]) != RunStatus.running.value:
    78	        raise PlannerError(
    79	            ErrorCode.stale_claim,
    80	            "run is not running",
    81	            {"run_id": run_id, "run_status": str(row["status"])},
    82	        )
    83	    ticket_id = str(row["ticket_id"])
    84	    ticket = conn.execute(
    85	        "SELECT claim_lock, claim_expires FROM tickets WHERE id=?", (ticket_id,)
    86	    ).fetchone()
    87	    claim_lock = str(ticket["claim_lock"]) if ticket["claim_lock"] is not None else None
    88	    claim_expires = int(ticket["claim_expires"]) if ticket["claim_expires"] is not None else None
    89	    if not has_active_claim(claim_lock, claim_expires, now):
    90	        raise PlannerError(
    91	            ErrorCode.stale_claim,
    92	            "claim expired or cleared",
    93	            {"ticket_id": ticket_id, "claim_expires": claim_expires},
    94	        )
    95	    new_expires = expiry_at(now, ttl_seconds)
    96	    conn.execute("UPDATE tickets SET claim_expires=? WHERE id=?", (new_expires, ticket_id))
    97	    append_event(
    98	        conn,
    99	        ticket_id,
   100	        EventKind.claim_heartbeat,
   101	        {"run_id": run_id, "claim_expires": new_expires},
   102	        now,
   103	    )
   104	    return new_expires
   105	
   106	
   107	class _Finalized(NamedTuple):
   108	    ticket_id: str
   109	    consecutive_failures: int
   110	    newly_auto_blocked: bool
   111	
   112	
   113	def _finalize_run(
   114	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   115	    run_id: str,
   116	    status: RunStatus,
   117	    now: int,
   118	    summary: str | None,
   119	    error: str | None,
   120	    failure_limit: int,
   121	) -> _Finalized | None:
   122	    """The single door for runs.status + breaker writes (D5). The runs-UPDATE is a
   123	    CAS guarded by status='running', so a close racing a reclaim has exactly one
   124	    winner; the loser gets rowcount 0 and None back. Returns None on a missing run or
   125	    a lost CAS — the caller decides what that means."""
   126	    row = conn.execute("SELECT ticket_id FROM runs WHERE id=?", (run_id,)).fetchone()
   127	    if row is None:
   128	        return None
   129	    ticket_id = str(row["ticket_id"])
   130	    cursor = conn.execute(
   131	        "UPDATE runs SET status=?, ended_at=?, summary=?, error=? WHERE id=? AND status='running'",
   132	        (status.value, now, summary, error, run_id),
   133	    )
   134	    if cursor.rowcount == 0:
   135	        return None
   136	    trow = conn.execute(
   137	        "SELECT consecutive_failures, auto_blocked FROM tickets WHERE id=?", (ticket_id,)
   138	    ).fetchone()
   139	    current = int(trow["consecutive_failures"])
   140	    was_blocked = bool(trow["auto_blocked"])
   141	    new_failures, tripped = next_breaker_state(current, status, failure_limit)
   142	    newly = tripped and not was_blocked
   143	    # auto_blocked is sticky: once set it is never written back to 0 here (only
   144	    # clear_auto_block does that).
   145	    flag = 1 if (was_blocked or tripped) else 0
   146	    # Clear the lease and apply the breaker in one statement (no updated_at, D7). The
   147	    # unconditional lock clear is safe: a newer claim needs claim_lock IS NULL, which
   148	    # needs this run finalized first — the CAS above established we are that finalizer.
   149	    conn.execute(
   150	        "UPDATE tickets SET claim_lock=NULL, claim_expires=NULL, "
   151	        "consecutive_failures=?, auto_blocked=? WHERE id=?",
   152	        (new_failures, flag, ticket_id),
   153	    )
   154	    return _Finalized(ticket_id, new_failures, newly)
   155	
   156	
   157	def close_run(
   158	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   159	    run_id: str,
   160	    status: RunStatus,
   161	    now: int,
   162	    failure_limit: int,
   163	    summary: str | None = None,
   164	    error: str | None = None,
   165	) -> None:
   166	    """Close a run with an agent outcome or failure status (§7.5). A1: an expired
   167	    claim can never write — the close is rejected (stale_claim) before finalizing,
   168	    leaving the run for sweep_reclaims. Emits run_closed, then auto_blocked only on the
   169	    breaker's 0->1 trip."""
   170	    if status not in _CLOSABLE:
   171	        raise PlannerError(
   172	            ErrorCode.validation, "not a closeable run status", {"status": status.value}
   173	        )
   174	    row = conn.execute("SELECT status, ticket_id FROM runs WHERE id=?", (run_id,)).fetchone()
   175	    if row is None:
   176	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "run not found", {"run_id": run_id})
   177	    current_status = str(row["status"])
   178	    if current_status != RunStatus.running.value:
   179	        raise PlannerError(
   180	            ErrorCode.stale_claim,
   181	            "run is not running",
   182	            {"run_id": run_id, "run_status": current_status},
   183	        )
   184	    ticket_id = str(row["ticket_id"])
   185	    ticket = conn.execute(
   186	        "SELECT claim_lock, claim_expires FROM tickets WHERE id=?", (ticket_id,)
   187	    ).fetchone()
   188	    claim_lock = str(ticket["claim_lock"]) if ticket["claim_lock"] is not None else None
   189	    claim_expires = int(ticket["claim_expires"]) if ticket["claim_expires"] is not None else None
   190	    if not has_active_claim(claim_lock, claim_expires, now):
   191	        raise PlannerError(
   192	            ErrorCode.stale_claim,
   193	            "claim expired or cleared",
   194	            {"ticket_id": ticket_id, "claim_expires": claim_expires},
   195	        )
   196	    fin = _finalize_run(conn, run_id, status, now, summary, error, failure_limit)
   197	    if fin is None:
   198	        raise PlannerError(
   199	            ErrorCode.stale_claim,
   200	            "run is not running",
   201	            {"run_id": run_id, "run_status": current_status},
   202	        )
   203	    append_event(
   204	        conn,
   205	        fin.ticket_id,
   206	        EventKind.run_closed,
   207	        {"run_id": run_id, "status": status.value, "summary": summary},
   208	        now,
   209	    )
   210	    if fin.newly_auto_blocked:
   211	        append_event(
   212	            conn,
   213	            fin.ticket_id,
   214	            EventKind.auto_blocked,
   215	            {"consecutive_failures": fin.consecutive_failures},
   216	            now,
   217	        )
   218	
   219	
   220	def sweep_reclaims(
   221	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   222	    now: int,
   223	    pid_alive: Callable[[int], bool],
   224	    failure_limit: int,
   225	) -> list[str]:
   226	    """Stage-4 tick primitive: reclaim every running run whose claim expired (reason
   227	    'expired', checked first — D14) or whose pid is dead (reason 'dead_pid'). Returns
   228	    the reclaimed run ids. reclaimed leaves the breaker unchanged (D6); a run whose
   229	    finalize CAS was lost to a legitimate close is skipped silently."""
   230	    rows = conn.execute(
   231	        "SELECT r.id AS run_id, r.pid, r.ticket_id, t.claim_expires "
   232	        "FROM runs r JOIN tickets t ON t.id = r.ticket_id "
   233	        "WHERE r.status = 'running' AND t.claim_lock IS NOT NULL"
   234	    ).fetchall()
   235	    reclaimed: list[str] = []
   236	    for row in rows:
   237	        run_id = str(row["run_id"])
   238	        ticket_id = str(row["ticket_id"])
   239	        claim_expires = int(row["claim_expires"]) if row["claim_expires"] is not None else None
   240	        pid = int(row["pid"]) if row["pid"] is not None else None
   241	        if is_expired(claim_expires, now):
   242	            reason = "expired"
   243	        elif pid is not None and not pid_alive(pid):
   244	            reason = "dead_pid"
   245	        else:
   246	            continue
   247	        fin = _finalize_run(conn, run_id, RunStatus.reclaimed, now, None, None, failure_limit)
   248	        if fin is None:
   249	            continue
   250	        append_event(
   251	            conn,
   252	            ticket_id,
   253	            EventKind.claim_reclaimed,
   254	            {"run_id": run_id, "reason": reason},
   255	            now,
   256	        )
   257	        reclaimed.append(run_id)
   258	    return reclaimed
   259	
   260	
   261	def clear_auto_block(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str, now: int) -> None:
   262	    """§7.5 human unblock: resets both the flag and the counter and bumps updated_at
   263	    (a human action on the ticket, D7). Idempotent — no guard on the current flag."""
   264	    row = conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM tickets WHERE id=?", (ticket_id,)).fetchone()
   265	    if row is None:
   266	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "ticket not found", {"ticket_id": ticket_id})
   267	    conn.execute(
   268	        "UPDATE tickets SET auto_blocked=0, consecutive_failures=0, updated_at=? WHERE id=?",
   269	        (now, ticket_id),
   270	    )
   271	    append_event(conn, ticket_id, EventKind.auto_block_cleared, {}, now)
   272	
   273	
   274	def load_candidates(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int) -> list[DispatchCandidate]:
   275	    """Assemble the pure §7.2 input rows for every ticket. Filtering is exclusively
   276	    logic.is_eligible's job — no eligibility rule is duplicated in SQL. Every row value
   277	    is coerced immediately so no Any leaks into the dataclass."""
   278	    blocked = blocked_target_ids(conn)
   279	    rows = conn.execute(
   280	        "SELECT id, state, priority, deadline, created_at, ceiling, at_cap, "
   281	        "auto_blocked, claim_lock, claim_expires, fields FROM tickets"
   282	    ).fetchall()
   283	    candidates: list[DispatchCandidate] = []
   284	    for row in rows:
   285	        state = TicketState(str(row["state"]))
   286	        raw_deadline = row["deadline"]
   287	        fields_json: JsonDict = json.loads(str(row["fields"]))
   288	        claim_lock = str(row["claim_lock"]) if row["claim_lock"] is not None else None
   289	        claim_expires = int(row["claim_expires"]) if row["claim_expires"] is not None else None
   290	        ticket_id = str(row["id"])
   291	        candidates.append(
   292	            DispatchCandidate(
   293	                ticket_id=ticket_id,
   294	                state=state,
   295	                priority=Priority(str(row["priority"])),
   296	                deadline=str(raw_deadline) if raw_deadline is not None else None,
   297	                created_at=int(row["created_at"]),
   298	                ceiling=TicketState(str(row["ceiling"])),
   299	                at_cap=AtCap(str(row["at_cap"])),
   300	                auto_blocked=bool(row["auto_blocked"]),
   301	                has_active_claim=has_active_claim(claim_lock, claim_expires, now),
   302	                is_blocked=ticket_id in blocked,
   303	                gating_pending=gating_field_pending(state, fields_json),
   304	            )
   305	        )
   306	    return candidates

/bin/zsh -lc 'nl -ba src/planner/dispatch/logic/eligibility.py' in /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""§7.2 dispatch eligibility over a pure DispatchCandidate, plus the gating-field
     2	pending check the data layer feeds it. Stdlib + contracts only; logic never touches
     3	the DB."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from typing import Final
     8	
     9	from planner.core.contracts import JsonDict
    10	from planner.dispatch.contracts import DispatchCandidate
    11	from planner.tickets.contracts import (
    12	    ADVANCE_TARGET,
    13	    GATING_FIELD,
    14	    STATE_ORDER,
    15	    AtCap,
    16	    TicketState,
    17	)
    18	
    19	# Position of each state in the linear pipeline; drives the "<= ceiling" comparison.
    20	_ORDER_INDEX: Final[dict[TicketState, int]] = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(STATE_ORDER)}
    21	
    22	
    23	def gating_field_pending(state: TicketState, fields: JsonDict) -> bool:
    24	    """True iff the state's gating field carries a pending proposal in the parsed
    25	    tickets.fields JSON. States without a gating field (needs_review, done, dropped)
    26	    return False."""
    27	    name = GATING_FIELD.get(state)
    28	    if name is None:
    29	        return False
    30	    slot = fields.get(name.value)
    31	    if not isinstance(slot, dict):
    32	        return False
    33	    return slot.get("proposal") is not None
    34	
    35	
    36	def is_eligible(candidate: DispatchCandidate) -> bool:
    37	    """§7.2 (SPEC line 149): the dispatch-eligibility conjunction plus the two
    38	    advance branches. Checks run in the order below; the terminal-state guard also
    39	    guarantees ADVANCE_TARGET[state] below never KeyErrors (the four remaining states
    40	    are exactly its keys)."""
    41	    if candidate.state in (
    42	        TicketState.done,
    43	        TicketState.dropped,
    44	        TicketState.needs_review,
    45	    ):
    46	        return False
    47	    if candidate.is_blocked:            # §3.6 line 57
    48	        return False
    49	    if candidate.auto_blocked:          # §7.5
    50	        return False
    51	    if candidate.has_active_claim:
    52	        return False
    53	    if candidate.gating_pending:        # current gating field has a pending proposal
    54	        return False
    55	    target = ADVANCE_TARGET[candidate.state]
    56	    if _ORDER_INDEX[target] <= _ORDER_INDEX[candidate.ceiling]:
    57	        return True                     # branch (a): the agent can move it
    58	    # branch (b): at ceiling + propose eligible; at ceiling + stop and past-ceiling not.
    59	    return candidate.state == candidate.ceiling and candidate.at_cap is AtCap.propose

    52	def resolve_day_id(date_seg: str, clock: Clock, config: Config) -> str:
    53	    """Amendment 10: `today` resolves through the planning date. A6: the ISO branch
    54	    parses then re-formats via ids.day_id, so compact forms ('20260704') still yield
    55	    canonical day_2026-07-04 ids — the raw segment is never string-formatted."""
    56	    if date_seg == "today":
    57	        return ids.day_id(planning_date(clock.now(), config.boundary_hour))
    58	    try:
    59	        parsed = date.fromisoformat(date_seg)
    60	    except ValueError:
    61	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid date", {"date": date_seg}) from None
    62	    return ids.day_id(parsed)

    64	
    65	def _day_view(conn: sqlite3.Connection, did: str, now: int) -> JsonDict:
    66	    # A2: read_day materializes an absent day (INSERT + event, multi-statement) on an
    67	    # autocommit connection, so this one read is wrapped; the rest run bare.
    68	    with txn(conn):
    69	        day = days_data.read_day(conn, did, now)
    70	    dts = days_data.list_day_tickets(conn, did)
    71	    return {
    72	        "id": day.id,
    73	        "brief": day.brief,
    74	        "notes": day.notes,
    75	        "plan": plan_tree.tree_to_dict(day.plan) if day.plan is not None else None,
    76	        "chat_session_key": day.chat_session_key,
    77	        "created_at": day.created_at,
    78	        "updated_at": day.updated_at,
    79	        "tickets": [ticket_json(read_ticket(conn, dt.ticket_id), now) for dt in dts],
    80	    }
    81	
    82	
    83	def _parse_node(raw: object) -> str | int:
    84	    if raw == "root":
    85	        return "root"
    86	    if isinstance(raw, int) and not isinstance(raw, bool):
    87	        return raw
    88	    raise PlannerError(
    89	        ErrorCode.validation, "node must be 'root' or a child position", {"node": raw}
    90	    )
    91	
    92	
    93	def _load_plan_or_error(conn: sqlite3.Connection, did: str) -> PlanTree:
    94	    plan = days_data.load_plan(conn, did)
    95	    if plan is None:
    96	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "day has no plan", {"day_id": did})
    97	    return plan
    98	
    99	
   100	def _require_child(tree: PlanTree, position: int) -> None:
   101	    if not any(child.position == position for child in tree.children):
   102	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no such plan node", {"node": position})
   103

   150	@router.post("/day/{date}/plan/accept")
   151	async def plan_accept(date: str, body: PlanNodeBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg,
   152	                      clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   153	    reject_agents(ctx)
   154	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   155	    now = clk.now_unix()
   156	    node = _parse_node(body.node)
   157	    tree = _load_plan_or_error(conn, did)
   158	    if isinstance(node, int):

   160	    new_tree, effects = plan_tree.accept_node(tree, node)
   161	    with txn(conn):

   163	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)

   168	    reject_agents(ctx)
   169	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   170	    now = clk.now_unix()
   171	    tree = _load_plan_or_error(conn, did)
   172	    new_tree, effects = plan_tree.accept_all(tree)
   173	    with txn(conn):
   174	        days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)

   179	async def plan_invalidate(date: str, body: PlanNodeBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg,
   180	                          clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   181	    reject_agents(ctx)
   182	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   183	    now = clk.now_unix()
   184	    node = _parse_node(body.node)
   185	    tree = _load_plan_or_error(conn, did)
   186	    if isinstance(node, int):
   187	        _require_child(tree, node)
   188	        new_tree, effects = plan_tree.invalidate_child(tree, node)
   189	    else:
   190	        new_tree, effects = plan_tree.invalidate_root(tree)
   191	    with txn(conn):
   192	        replans = days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)
   193	    for request in replans:  # post-commit enqueue: the R5 consumer sees committed state only
   194	        submit_replan(did, request)
   195	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)
   196	
   197	
   198	@router.post("/day/{date}/plan/reject-all")
   199	async def plan_reject_all(date: str, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   200	    reject_agents(ctx)
   201	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   202	    now = clk.now_unix()
   203	    tree = _load_plan_or_error(conn, did)
   204	    new_tree, effects = plan_tree.reject_all(tree)
   205	    with txn(conn):
   206	        days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)
   207	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""§7.2 dispatch ordering key: priority band, then deadline (ascending, NULLs last),
     2	then created_at ascending. Stdlib + contracts only; callers compose the sort themselves
     3	(sorted(..., key=ordering_key)) so this module imports no sibling logic module."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from typing import Final
     8	
     9	from planner.core.contracts import Priority
    10	from planner.dispatch.contracts import DispatchCandidate
    11	
    12	_PRIORITY_RANK: Final[dict[Priority, int]] = {
    13	    Priority.P0: 0,
    14	    Priority.P1: 1,
    15	    Priority.P2: 2,
    16	    Priority.P3: 3,
    17	}
    18	
    19	
    20	def ordering_key(candidate: DispatchCandidate) -> tuple[int, int, str, int]:
    21	    """§7.2 ordering: priority (P0 first), deadline ascending with NULLs last,
    22	    created_at ascending. ISO date strings compare lexicographically ==
    23	    chronologically; the NULL flag (0 dated / 1 undated) puts every dated ticket
    24	    before every undated one regardless of the string component."""
    25	    return (
    26	        _PRIORITY_RANK[candidate.priority],
    27	        0 if candidate.deadline is not None else 1,
    28	        candidate.deadline or "",
    29	        candidate.created_at,
    30	    )

     6	FastAPI appears here because this is part of the server shell (the server.py family)."""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import importlib
    11	import sqlite3
    12	from collections.abc import Callable
    13	from typing import Any
    14

    18	from planner.core.adapters.registry import Adapters

    20	from planner.core.config import Config

    26	def _not_wired() -> JSONResponse:
    27	    # Returned directly (never raised as a PlannerError, which the handler would map
    28	    # to 400): the seam pins this exact body for T11's tests to compare whole.
    29	    return JSONResponse(
    30	        status_code=501,
    31	        content={"error": {"code": "validation", "message": "runtime not wired yet"}},
    32	    )
    33	
    34	
    35	def _lazy(module_name: str, attr: str) -> Any | None:
    36	    # importlib (not a from-import) keeps the not-yet-existing T11 modules out of the
    37	    # static import graph; ImportError/AttributeError are the seam's named failures.
    38	    try:
    39	        module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
    40	        return getattr(module, attr)
    41	    except (ImportError, AttributeError):
    42	        return None
    43	
    44	
    45	def build_test_router(
    46	    config: Config,
    47	    clock: Clock,
    48	    adapters: Adapters,
    49	    conn_factory: Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection],

    51	    router = APIRouter()

    53	    @router.post("/test/set-now")
    54	    async def set_now(body: dict[str, Any]) -> JsonDict:
    55	        raw = body.get("now")
    56	        if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
    57	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "body must carry an ISO 'now' string")
    58	        try:
    59	            parsed = parse_fake_now(raw)
    60	        except ValueError as exc:
    61	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid ISO datetime: {raw!r}") from exc
    62	        if not isinstance(clock, TestClock):
    63	            raise PlannerError(
    64	                ErrorCode.validation, "clock is not a TestClock; start with PLAN_FAKE_NOW set"
    65	            )
    66	        clock.set(parsed)
    67	        return {
    68	            "now": clock.now().isoformat(),
    69	            "planning_date": planning_date(clock.now(), config.boundary_hour).isoformat(),
    70	        }
    71	
    72	    @router.post("/test/tick-boundary")
    73	    async def tick_boundary() -> JSONResponse:
    74	        fn = _lazy("planner.days.scheduler", "run_boundary_tick")
    75	        if fn is None:
    76	            return _not_wired()
    77	        report = fn(conn_factory, config, clock, adapters)
    78	        return JSONResponse(status_code=200, content=report)
    79	
    80	    @router.post("/test/tick-dispatcher")
    81	    async def tick_dispatcher() -> JSONResponse:
    82	        fn = _lazy("planner.dispatch.runtime", "run_tick")
    83	        if fn is None:
    84	            return _not_wired()
    85	        report = fn(conn_factory, config, clock, adapters)
    86	        return JSONResponse(status_code=200, content=report)

    88	    return router

    23	def seed_demo(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    24	    for table in _TABLES:
    25	        if conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {table}").fetchone()[0] > 0:
    26	            raise PlannerError(
    27	                ErrorCode.db_not_empty, "seed --demo requires an empty database", {"table": table}
    28	            )

    33	        _build_demo(conn, now, today)
    34	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
    35	    except Exception:
    36	        conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
