   115	
   116	def _insert_sprint(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int, today: date) -> str:
   117	    sprint_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["sprint"])
   118	    date_start = (today - timedelta(days=3)).isoformat()
   119	    date_end = (today + timedelta(days=10)).isoformat()
   120	    conn.execute(
   121	        "INSERT INTO sprints (id, name, date_start, date_end, limiting_factor, primary_bet, "
   122	        "supports, premortem, created_at, updated_at) "
   123	        "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
   124	        (
   125	            sprint_id, "Demo Sprint", date_start, date_end,
   126	            "Demo: one clear slice must ship.",
   127	            "Demo: shipping the feature slice unblocks the review queue.",
   128	            "- Demo support line.", "- Demo premortem line.", now, now,
   129	        ),
   130	    )
   131	    append_event(
   132	        conn, sprint_id, EventKind.sprint_created,
   133	        {"name": "Demo Sprint", "date_start": date_start, "date_end": date_end, "source": "demo"},
   134	        now,
   135	    )
   136	    return sprint_id
   137	
   138	
   139	def _insert_item(
   140	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   141	    sprint_id: str,
   142	    title: str,
   143	    status: str,
   144	    priority: str,
   145	    project: str,
   146	    body: str,
   147	    now: int,
   148	) -> str:
   149	    item_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["sprint_item"])
   150	    conn.execute(
   151	        "INSERT INTO sprint_items (id, title, body, status, priority, deadline, project, "
   152	        "current_state_note, sprint_id, created_at, updated_at) "
   153	        "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
   154	        (item_id, title, body, status, priority, None, project, "", sprint_id, now, now),
   155	    )
   156	    append_event(
   157	        conn, item_id, EventKind.sprint_item_created,
   158	        {"title": title, "status": status, "sprint_id": sprint_id, "source": "demo"},
   159	        now,
   160	    )
   161	    return item_id
   162	
   163	
   164	def _fields(title: str, keys: tuple[str, ...], result_notes: str | None = None) -> str:
   165	    slots: dict[str, dict[str, str | None]] = {
   166	        name: {"value": None, "proposal": None, "notes": None}
   167	        for name in ("success", "approach", "plan", "result")
   168	    }
   169	    for key in keys:
   170	        slots[key]["value"] = f"Demo {key} for {title}"
   171	    if result_notes is not None:
   172	        slots["result"]["notes"] = result_notes
   173	    return json.dumps(slots)
   174	
   175	
   176	def _insert_ticket(
   177	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   178	    now: int,
   179	    *,
   180	    title: str,
   181	    state: str,
   182	    priority: str,
   183	    deadline: str | None,
   184	    ceiling: str,
   185	    at_cap: str,
   186	    sprint_item_id: str | None,
   187	    sprint_id: str | None,
   188	    project: str | None,
   189	    fields: str,
   190	) -> str:
   191	    ticket_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["ticket"])
   192	    conn.execute(
   193	        "INSERT INTO tickets (id, title, state, priority, deadline, project, sprint_item_id, "
   194	        "sprint_id, recap, ceiling, at_cap, auto_blocked, consecutive_failures, "
   195	        "chat_session_key, alias, fields, created_at, updated_at) "
   196	        "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
   197	        (
   198	            ticket_id, title, state, priority, deadline, project, sprint_item_id, sprint_id,
   199	            "", ceiling, at_cap, 0, 0, None, None, fields, now, now,
   200	        ),
   201	    )
   202	    append_event(
   203	        conn, ticket_id, EventKind.ticket_created,
   204	        {"title": title, "state": state, "alias": None, "source": "demo"},
   205	        now,
   206	    )
   207	    return ticket_id
   208	
   209	
   210	def _insert_link(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int, from_id: str, to_id: str, kind: str) -> None:
   211	    conn.execute(
   212	        "INSERT INTO links (from_id, to_id, kind) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", (from_id, to_id, kind)
   213	    )
   214	    append_event(
   215	        conn, from_id, EventKind.link_added,
   216	        {"from_id": from_id, "to_id": to_id, "kind": kind, "source": "demo"},
   217	        now,
   218	    )
   219	
   220	
   221	def _insert_day(
   222	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int, today: date, t4: str, t8: str, t5: str
   223	) -> None:
   224	    did = day_id(today)
   225	    plan_tree = {
   226	        "root": {"focus": "Ship the demo feature slice.", "status": "accepted"},
   227	        "children": [
   228	            {
   229	                "ticket_id": t4, "note": "Land the slice behind the flag.",
   230	                "status": "accepted", "position": 0,
   231	            },
   232	            {
   233	                "ticket_id": t8, "note": "Stabilise the login test before review.",
   234	                "status": "proposed", "position": 1,
   235	            },
   236	        ],
   237	    }
   238	    conn.execute(
   239	        "INSERT INTO days (id, brief, notes, plan, chat_session_key, created_at, updated_at) "
   240	        "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
   241	        (
   242	            did, "Demo day: ship the feature slice and clear the review queue.", "",
   243	            json.dumps(plan_tree), None, now, now,
   244	        ),
   245	    )
   246	    append_event(conn, did, EventKind.day_created, {"source": "demo"}, now)
   247	    for ticket_id, position in ((t4, 0), (t8, 1), (t5, 2)):
   248	        conn.execute(
   249	            "INSERT INTO day_tickets (day_id, ticket_id, position) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
   250	            (did, ticket_id, position),
   251	        )
   252	        append_event(
   253	            conn, did, EventKind.day_ticket_added,
   254	            {"ticket_id": ticket_id, "position": position, "cause": "demo", "source": "demo"},
   255	            now,
   256	        )

     1	"""E2E harness fixtures (SPEC §18.3 items 22-27). Standalone: imports nothing from
     2	tests/unit.
     3	
     4	Each test gets a real ``plan serve`` subprocess on an OS-assigned port, backed by a
     5	fresh temp SQLite DB in ``PLAN_TEST_MODE`` with the boundaries faked (echo gateway by
     6	default; ``server_factory(gateway="offline")`` boots a second instance for the offline
     7	notice). Browser contexts come from pytest-playwright's session ``browser``; the
     8	``open_page`` / ``cli`` / ``api`` helpers drive the surfaces. Every Playwright wait
     9	carries an explicit ``timeout``; the only sleep is the readiness poll's 0.1s interval,
    10	which polls a condition inside a boot budget.
    11	"""
    12	
    13	from __future__ import annotations
    14	
    15	import json
    16	import os
    17	import socket
    18	import subprocess
    19	import time
    20	from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator
    21	from dataclasses import dataclass
    22	from pathlib import Path
    23	from types import SimpleNamespace
    24	
    25	import httpx
    26	import pytest
    27	from playwright.sync_api import Browser, BrowserContext, Page
    28	
    29	REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    30	PLAN_BIN = REPO_ROOT / ".venv" / "bin" / "plan"
    31	FAKE_NOW = "2026-07-04T12:00:00"
    32	WAIT_MS = 10_000          # every Playwright wait
    33	BOOT_BUDGET_S = 15.0      # server readiness budget
    34	
    35	
    36	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    37	class ServerHandle:
    38	    base: str
    39	    proc: subprocess.Popen[bytes]
    40	    db_path: Path
    41	    log_path: Path
    42	
    43	
    44	def _free_port() -> int:
    45	    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
    46	        sock.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
    47	        return int(sock.getsockname()[1])
    48	
    49	
    50	def _scrubbed_env() -> dict[str, str]:
    51	    """os.environ minus every ambient PLAN_* key (kills shell leakage)."""
    52	    return {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith("PLAN_")}
    53	
    54	
    55	def _log_tail(log_path: Path, n: int = 40) -> str:
    56	    try:
    57	        lines = log_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines()
    58	    except OSError:
    59	        return "(no server log)"
    60	    return "\n".join(lines[-n:])
    61	
    62	
    63	@pytest.fixture
    64	def server_factory(tmp_path: Path) -> Iterator[Callable[..., ServerHandle]]:
    65	    handles: list[ServerHandle] = []
    66	    counter = 0
    67	
    68	    def make(gateway: str | None = None) -> ServerHandle:
    69	        nonlocal counter
    70	        srvdir = tmp_path / f"srv{counter}"
    71	        counter += 1
    72	        # A1: the log file is opened from THIS process before the subprocess exists;
    73	        # plan serve only creates directories later, inside itself.
    74	        srvdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    75	
    76	        port = _free_port()
    77	        base = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"
    78	        db_path = srvdir / "planning.db"
    79	        log_path = srvdir / "server.log"
    80	
    81	        env = _scrubbed_env()
    82	        env.update(
    83	            {
    84	                "PLAN_TEST_MODE": "1",
    85	                "PLAN_DB_PATH": str(db_path),
    86	                "PLAN_PORT": str(port),
    87	                "PLAN_FAKE_NOW": FAKE_NOW,
    88	                "PLAN_LOGS_DIR": str(srvdir / "logs"),
    89	                "PLAN_DISPATCHER_LOCK_PATH": str(srvdir / "dispatcher.lock"),
    90	                "PLAN_WS_POLL_MS": "50",
    91	                "PLAN_UI_DEBOUNCE_MS": "50",
    92	            }
    93	        )
    94	        if gateway == "offline":
    95	            env["PLAN_GATEWAY_ADAPTER"] = "offline"
    96	
    97	        log = log_path.open("wb")
    98	        try:
    99	            proc = subprocess.Popen(
   100	                [str(PLAN_BIN), "serve"],
   101	                cwd=str(REPO_ROOT),           # load-bearing: assets mount + config.yaml
   102	                env=env,
   103	                stdout=log,
   104	                stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
   105	            )
   106	        finally:
   107	            log.close()                       # the child keeps its own dup'd fd
   108	        handle = ServerHandle(base=base, proc=proc, db_path=db_path, log_path=log_path)
   109	        handles.append(handle)
   110	
   111	        deadline = time.time() + BOOT_BUDGET_S
   112	        while time.time() < deadline:
   113	            if proc.poll() is not None:
   114	                raise AssertionError(
   115	                    f"plan serve exited during boot (rc={proc.returncode})\n"
   116	                    f"--- server log tail ---\n{_log_tail(log_path)}"
   117	                )
   118	            try:
   119	                resp: httpx.Response | None = httpx.get(f"{base}/api/meta", timeout=1.0)
   120	            except httpx.HTTPError:
   121	                resp = None
   122	            if resp is not None and resp.status_code == 200:
   123	                meta = resp.json()
   124	                assert meta["test_mode"] is True, meta
   125	                assert meta["ui_debounce_ms"] == 50, meta
   126	                assets = httpx.get(f"{base}/assets/api.js", timeout=1.0)
   127	                assert assets.status_code == 200, assets.status_code
   128	                return handle
   129	            time.sleep(0.1)
   130	
   131	        proc.terminate()
   132	        raise AssertionError(
   133	            f"plan serve did not become ready within {BOOT_BUDGET_S}s\n"
   134	            f"--- server log tail ---\n{_log_tail(log_path)}"
   135	        )
   136	
   137	    yield make
   138	
   139	    for handle in handles:
   140	        handle.proc.terminate()
   141	        try:
   142	            handle.proc.wait(timeout=5)
   143	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   144	            handle.proc.kill()
   145	            handle.proc.wait()
   146

   169	@pytest.fixture
   170	def open_page() -> Callable[..., Page]:
   171	    def _open(
   172	        ctx: BrowserContext,
   173	        server: ServerHandle,
   174	        route: str,
   175	        ready_selector: str,
   176	        settled: bool,
   177	    ) -> Page:
   178	        page = ctx.new_page()
   179	        page.goto(server.base + "/" + route)
   180	        page.wait_for_selector(ready_selector, timeout=WAIT_MS)
   181	        # WS-open gate: never fire an observed mutation before the socket is live.
   182	        page.wait_for_function(
   183	            "() => window.__plannerDebug && window.__plannerDebug.wsOpens >= 1",
   184	            timeout=WAIT_MS,
   185	        )
   186	        if settled:
   187	            # Events preceded page-open: let the since=0 catch-up replay's one flush
   188	            # re-render land, then re-anchor on the ready selector (screen replaced).
   189	            page.wait_for_function(
   190	                "() => window.__plannerDebug && window.__plannerDebug.flushes >= 1",
   191	                timeout=WAIT_MS,
   192	            )
   193	            page.wait_for_selector(ready_selector, timeout=WAIT_MS)
   194	        return page
   195	
   196	    return _open

   237	@pytest.fixture

   239	    def get(server: ServerHandle, path: str) -> dict:
   240	        resp = httpx.get(server.base + path, timeout=10.0)
   241	        assert resp.status_code < 300, f"GET {path} -> {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}"
   242	        return resp.json()
   243	
   244	    def human_post(server: ServerHandle, path: str, json_body: dict) -> dict:
   245	        # No X-Plan-* headers: authctx classifies a header-less request as the human,
   246	        # which is what /grant and /accept require.
   247	        resp = httpx.post(server.base + path, json=json_body, timeout=10.0)
   248	        assert resp.status_code < 300, f"POST {path} -> {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}"
   249	        return resp.json()
   250

    68	    Item(23, "e2e", "test_e23", "env-pinned propose"),
    69	    Item(24, "e2e", "test_e24", "accept in Review"),
    70	    Item(25, "e2e", "test_e25", "edit-accept in Review"),
    71	    Item(26, "e2e", "test_e26", "chat panel"),
    72	    Item(27, "e2e", "test_e27", "auto-accept chain e2e"),
    73	    Item(28, "e2e", "test_e28", "day view"),
    74	    Item(29, "e2e", "test_e29", "invalidation"),
    75	    Item(30, "e2e", "test_e30", "dispatcher e2e"),
    76	    Item(31, "e2e", "test_e31", "refresh restores state"),
    77	    Item(32, "e2e", "test_e32", "sprint view live"),
    78	    Item(33, "e2e", "test_e33", "seed e2e"),
    79	    Item(34, "e2e", "test_e34", "snapshot migration e2e"),
    80	    Item(35, "e2e", "test_e35", "dogfood Level A"),
    81	    Item(36, "unit", "test_a36", "onward grant"),
    82	]
    83	
    84	
    85	# --- skip-scan -------------------------------------------------------------
    86	
    87	# Substring patterns forbidden anywhere in a test line (SPEC 18.2). Note that
    88	# "@pytest.mark.skip" also catches "@pytest.mark.skipif" and "xfail" catches
    89	# both the marker and pytest.xfail() — stricter, never weaker.
    90	_TEXT_PATTERNS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    91	    "@pytest.mark.skip",
    92	    "pytest.skip(",
    93	    "xfail",
    94	    ".only",
    95	)
    96	
    97	
    98	def _iter_py_files(paths: Iterable[Path | str]) -> Iterator[Path]:
    99	    """Yield every *.py file for the given paths; a path may be a file or a dir."""
   100	    for raw in paths:
   101	        p = Path(raw)
   102	        if p.is_dir():
   103	            yield from sorted(p.rglob("*.py"))
   104	        elif p.suffix == ".py" and p.is_file():
   105	            yield p
   106	
   107	
   108	def _is_empty_test_body(fn: ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef) -> bool:
   109	    """True when a test function's body is only pass / ... / string literals."""
   110	    body = list(fn.body)
   111	    for stmt in body:
   112	        if isinstance(stmt, ast.Pass):
   113	            continue
   114	        if isinstance(stmt, ast.Expr) and isinstance(stmt.value, ast.Constant):
   115	            # a bare docstring, "..." (Ellipsis), or string literal is a no-op
   116	            if stmt.value.value is Ellipsis or isinstance(stmt.value.value, str):
   117	                continue
   118	        return False
   119	    return True
   120	
   121	
   122	def scan_test_files(paths: Iterable[Path | str]) -> list[Violation]:
   123	    """Scan test files for the six forbidden patterns of SPEC 18.2.
   124	
   125	    Returns a deduplicated (file, pattern) list. Files that cannot be read are
   126	    skipped; a file that will not parse still gets its text patterns scanned.
   127	    """
   128	    violations: list[Violation] = []
   129	    seen: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
   130	
   131	    def add(file_str: str, pattern: str) -> None:
   132	        key = (file_str, pattern)
   133	        if key not in seen:
   134	            seen.add(key)
   135	            violations.append(Violation(file_str, pattern))
   136	
   137	    for path in _iter_py_files(paths):
   138	        try:
   139	            text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   140	        except OSError:
   141	            continue
   142	        file_str = str(path)
   143	
   144	        for line in text.splitlines():
   145	            for pattern in _TEXT_PATTERNS:
   146	                if pattern in line:
   147	                    add(file_str, pattern)
   148	            stripped = line.strip()
   149	            if stripped.startswith("#") and "def test_" in stripped:
   150	                add(file_str, "commented-out test")
   151	
   152	        try:
   153	            tree = ast.parse(text)
   154	        except SyntaxError:
   155	            continue
   156	        for node in ast.walk(tree):
   157	            if (
   158	                isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef))
   159	                and node.name.startswith("test_")
   160	                and _is_empty_test_body(node)
   161	            ):
   162	                add(file_str, "empty test body")
   163	
   164	    return violations
   165	
   166	
   167	# --- junit parsing + scoring ----------------------------------------------
   168	
   169	
   170	def _outcome(testcase: ElementTree.Element) -> str:
   171	    """Map a junit <testcase> to passed / failed / error / skipped."""
   172	    tags = {child.tag.lower() for child in testcase}
   173	    if "error" in tags:
   174	        return "error"
   175	    if "failure" in tags:
   176	        return "failed"
   177	    if "skipped" in tags:
   178	        return "skipped"
   179	    return "passed"
   180	
   181	
   182	def parse_junit(path: Path | str) -> dict[str, str]:
   183	    """Parse a junit XML file into {classname::name: outcome}.
   184	
   185	    A missing or unparseable file yields an empty map, so every item scored
   186	    against it FAILs. Keys are fully qualified so same-named tests in different
   187	    files stay distinct (and thus count as multiple matches).
   188	    """
   189	    p = Path(path)
   190	    if not p.exists():
   191	        return {}
   192	    try:
   193	        tree = ElementTree.parse(p)
   194	    except (ElementTree.ParseError, OSError):
   195	        return {}
   196	    results: dict[str, str] = {}
   197	    for testcase in tree.iter("testcase"):
   198	        name = testcase.get("name")
   199	        if not name:
   200	            continue
   201	        classname = testcase.get("classname", "")
   202	        results[f"{classname}::{name}"] = _outcome(testcase)
   203	    return results
   204	
   205	
   206	def score(
   207	    unit_results: dict[str, str], e2e_results: dict[str, str]
   208	) -> list[ItemResult]:
   209	    """Score every item. An item PASSes iff exactly one collected test name is
   210	    anchored to its token (bare name starts with ``<token>_``) and that test
   211	    passed; zero matches, multiple matches, failure, error, or skip mean FAIL.
   212	    """
   213	    out: list[ItemResult] = []
   214	    for item in ITEMS:
   215	        table = unit_results if item.suite == "unit" else e2e_results
   216	        prefix = item.token + "_"
   217	        matches = [
   218	            outcome
   219	            for key, outcome in table.items()
   220	            if key.rsplit("::", 1)[-1].startswith(prefix)
   221	        ]
   222	        passed = len(matches) == 1 and matches[0] == "passed"
   223	        out.append(ItemResult(item, passed))
   224	    return out

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""The verify instrument — the single source of truth for completeness (SPEC 18.2).
     2	
     3	Runs a preflight skip-scan of tests/, then the code and test gates in the spec's
     4	stated order (ruff, mypy, unit suite, build check, e2e suite), streaming each
     5	gate's output live. Ends with a 36-item scoreboard and exactly one final line,
     6	``VERIFY: N/36 PASS``. Exit is 0 iff the scan is clean, every gate passed, and
     7	N == 36.
     8	
     9	Documented limitation: this instrument proves the named tests RAN and PASSED. It
    10	does not judge assertion strength — that is enforced by the SPEC 18.3 fences
    11	(exact states, orderings, and error shapes) and the independent codex audit, not
    12	here.
    13	"""
    14	
    15	from __future__ import annotations
    16	
    17	import subprocess
    18	import sys
    19	from dataclasses import dataclass
    20	from pathlib import Path
    21	
    22	from verify_lib import ITEMS, ItemResult, parse_junit, scan_test_files, score
    23	
    24	REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
    25	VENV_BIN = REPO_ROOT / ".venv" / "bin"
    26	DATA_VERIFY = REPO_ROOT / "data" / "verify"
    27	
    28	# Generous ceiling so a hung server can never wedge verify forever.
    29	E2E_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 1800
    30	
    31	
    32	@dataclass
    33	class GateResult:
    34	    name: str
    35	    ok: bool
    36	    detail: str = ""
    37	
    38	
    39	def _run(cmd: list[str], timeout: int | None = None) -> tuple[int, bool]:
    40	    """Run a gate command with inherited stdio (live streaming).
    41	
    42	    Returns (returncode, timed_out). Never raises: a missing command or a
    43	    timeout is reported as a failed gate, not a crash.
    44	    """
    45	    printable = " ".join(str(c) for c in cmd)
    46	    print(f"\n=== {printable} ===", flush=True)
    47	    try:
    48	        proc = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=REPO_ROOT, timeout=timeout)
    49	        return proc.returncode, False
    50	    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
    51	        print(f"[verify] gate timed out after {timeout}s and was killed", flush=True)
    52	        return -1, True
    53	    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
    54	        print(f"[verify] gate command not found: {exc}", flush=True)
    55	        return -1, False
    56	
    57	
    58	def run_ruff() -> GateResult:
    59	    rc, _ = _run([str(VENV_BIN / "ruff"), "check", "."])
    60	    return GateResult("ruff", rc == 0)
    61	
    62	
    63	def run_mypy() -> GateResult:
    64	    rc, _ = _run([str(VENV_BIN / "mypy"), "src/"])
    65	    return GateResult("mypy", rc == 0)
    66	
    67	
    68	def run_pytest(target: str, junit: Path, name: str, timeout: int | None = None) -> GateResult:
    69	    """Run a pytest suite, writing junit XML for scoring.
    70	
    71	    A stale junit from a prior run is removed first so a crash or timeout that
    72	    writes nothing scores the suite's items FAIL rather than reusing old data.
    73	    Exit code 5 (no tests collected) is tolerated at stage 2: the gate is not
    74	    green, but it is not a crash either.
    75	    """
    76	    if junit.exists():
    77	        junit.unlink()
    78	    cmd = [str(VENV_BIN / "pytest"), target, f"--junitxml={junit}"]
    79	    rc, timed_out = _run(cmd, timeout=timeout)
    80	    if timed_out:
    81	        return GateResult(name, False, "timed out")
    82	    if rc == 5:
    83	        return GateResult(name, False, "no tests collected")
    84	    return GateResult(name, rc == 0)
    85	
    86	
    87	def run_build_check() -> GateResult:
    88	    """compileall over src/ plus node --check on every *.js under assets/."""
    89	    ok = True
    90	    rc, _ = _run([sys.executable, "-m", "compileall", "-q", "src/"])
    91	    if rc != 0:
    92	        ok = False
    93	    assets = REPO_ROOT / "assets"
    94	    js_files = sorted(assets.rglob("*.js")) if assets.is_dir() else []
    95	    for js in js_files:
    96	        rc_js, _ = _run(["node", "--check", str(js)])
    97	        if rc_js != 0:
    98	            ok = False
    99	    return GateResult("build check", ok)
   100	
   101	
   102	def print_scoreboard(results: list[ItemResult]) -> None:
   103	    for r in results:
   104	        tag = "PASS" if r.passed else "FAIL"
   105	        print(f"[{tag}] item {r.item.number:02d} — {r.item.label}")
   106	
   107	
   108	def main() -> int:
   109	    DATA_VERIFY.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   110	
   111	    # Preflight skip-scan. A tainted suite has no valid results, so on any
   112	    # violation we name each file+pattern, skip the suites, and still print the
   113	    # full all-FAIL scoreboard.
   114	    violations = scan_test_files([REPO_ROOT / "tests"])
   115	    if violations:
   116	        print("[verify] SKIP-SCAN FAILED — forbidden patterns in tests/:", flush=True)
   117	        for v in violations:
   118	            print(f"    {v.file}: {v.pattern}", flush=True)
   119	        print(
   120	            "[verify] test suites skipped; a tainted suite has no valid results.",
   121	            flush=True,
   122	        )
   123	        print_scoreboard([ItemResult(item, False) for item in ITEMS])
   124	        print("VERIFY: 0/36 PASS")
   125	        return 1
   126	
   127	    gates: list[GateResult] = []
   128	    gates.append(run_ruff())
   129	    gates.append(run_mypy())
   130	
   131	    unit_junit = DATA_VERIFY / "unit.xml"
   132	    gates.append(run_pytest("tests/unit", unit_junit, "unit suite"))
   133	
   134	    gates.append(run_build_check())
   135	
   136	    e2e_junit = DATA_VERIFY / "e2e.xml"
   137	    gates.append(
   138	        run_pytest("tests/e2e", e2e_junit, "e2e suite", timeout=E2E_TIMEOUT_SECONDS)
   139	    )
   140	
   141	    results = score(parse_junit(unit_junit), parse_junit(e2e_junit))
   142	
   143	    print()
   144	    for gate in gates:
   145	        status = "ok" if gate.ok else "FAILED"
   146	        suffix = f" ({gate.detail})" if gate.detail else ""
   147	        print(f"[verify] gate {gate.name}: {status}{suffix}")
   148	    print()
   149	
   150	    print_scoreboard(results)
   151	    passed = sum(1 for r in results if r.passed)
   152	    print(f"VERIFY: {passed}/36 PASS")
   153	
   154	    ok = all(gate.ok for gate in gates) and passed == len(ITEMS)
   155	    return 0 if ok else 1
   156	
   157	
   158	if __name__ == "__main__":
   159	    assert len(ITEMS) == 36, f"item registry must hold 36 items, has {len(ITEMS)}"
   160	    sys.exit(main())

     1	"""Adapter registry (§9.4). Selects real vs fake (vs offline gateway) per adapter
     2	from config keys. `auto` resolves to fake under test mode and real otherwise;
     3	explicit values always win. An unknown value fails loudly at startup."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from dataclasses import dataclass
     8	from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
     9	
    10	from planner.core.adapters.base import BoundaryAdapter, GatewayAdapter, SpawnAdapter
    11	from planner.core.adapters.fakes import (
    12	    EchoGatewayAdapter,
    13	    FakeBoundaryAdapter,
    14	    FakeSpawnAdapter,
    15	    OfflineGatewayAdapter,
    16	)
    17	from planner.core.adapters.real import (
    18	    RealBoundaryAdapter,
    19	    RealGatewayAdapter,
    20	    RealSpawnAdapter,
    21	)
    22	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    23	
    24	if TYPE_CHECKING:
    25	    from planner.core.config import Config
    26	
    27	
    28	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    29	class Adapters:
    30	    spawn: SpawnAdapter
    31	    boundary: BoundaryAdapter
    32	    gateway: GatewayAdapter
    33	
    34	
    35	def _resolve_auto(choice: str, test_mode: bool) -> str:
    36	    if choice == "auto":
    37	        return "fake" if test_mode else "real"
    38	    return choice
    39	
    40	
    41	def _select_spawn(config: Config) -> SpawnAdapter:
    42	    choice = _resolve_auto(config.spawn_adapter, config.test_mode)
    43	    if choice == "fake":
    44	        return FakeSpawnAdapter()
    45	    if choice == "real":
    46	        return RealSpawnAdapter(config)
    47	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"unknown spawn_adapter: {config.spawn_adapter!r}")
    48	
    49	
    50	def _select_boundary(config: Config) -> BoundaryAdapter:
    51	    choice = _resolve_auto(config.boundary_adapter, config.test_mode)
    52	    if choice == "fake":
    53	        return FakeBoundaryAdapter()
    54	    if choice == "real":
    55	        return RealBoundaryAdapter(config)
    56	    raise PlannerError(
    57	        ErrorCode.validation, f"unknown boundary_adapter: {config.boundary_adapter!r}"
    58	    )
    59	
    60	
    61	def _select_gateway(config: Config) -> GatewayAdapter:
    62	    choice = _resolve_auto(config.gateway_adapter, config.test_mode)
    63	    if choice == "fake":
    64	        return EchoGatewayAdapter()
    65	    if choice == "offline":
    66	        return OfflineGatewayAdapter()
    67	    if choice == "real":
    68	        return RealGatewayAdapter(config)
    69	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"unknown gateway_adapter: {config.gateway_adapter!r}")
    70	
    71	
    72	def build_adapters(config: Config) -> Adapters:
    73	    return Adapters(
    74	        spawn=_select_spawn(config),
    75	        boundary=_select_boundary(config),
    76	        gateway=_select_gateway(config),
    77	    )

     1	"""FastAPI app factory shell. Mounts the domain routers under /api, serves the
     2	static assets and the app shell, exposes GET /api/meta and the event WebSocket,
     3	and — only in test mode — the /api/test/* control endpoints. The resolution
     4	engine, dispatcher, and real route bodies land in later stages; here the surface

     6	
     7	This module and the domain api.py files are the only places FastAPI/pydantic

     9

    11

    13	import logging
    14	import sqlite3

    17	from pathlib import Path
    18	from typing import Any

    67	</html>
    68	"""
    69	
    70	
    71	def http_status_for(code: ErrorCode) -> int:
    72	    return _STATUS_BY_CODE.get(code, 400)
    73	
    74	
    75	def create_app(
    76	    config: Config,
    77	    clock: Clock,
    78	    adapters: Adapters,
    79	    conn_factory: Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection],
    80	) -> FastAPI:
    81	    # The DDL carries the literal 200 title cap; the config value is what write
    82	    # paths enforce. Guard that they agree (amendment 11). Factory time is startup
    83	    # and strictly earlier than any request, so this fires even for TestClient apps
    84	    # that never enter the lifespan.
    85	    assert config.title_max_chars == 200, "title_max_chars must equal the DDL literal (200)"
    86	
    87	    @asynccontextmanager
    88	    async def _lifespan(app_: FastAPI) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
    89	        Path(config.db_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    90	        Path(config.logs_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    91	        loops: Any = None
    92	        if not config.test_mode:  # D6: background loops never run in test mode
    93	            try:
    94	                module = importlib.import_module("planner.core.loops")
    95	                start = module.start_background_loops
    96	            except (ImportError, AttributeError):
    97	                _log.warning("planner.core.loops unavailable; running without background loops")
    98	            else:
    99	                loops = start(config, clock, adapters, conn_factory)
   100	        try:
   101	            yield
   102	        finally:
   103	            if loops is not None:
   104	                await loops.stop()
   105	
   106	    app = FastAPI(title="planner", version="2.0.0", lifespan=_lifespan)
   107	    app.state.config = config
   108	    app.state.clock = clock
   109	    app.state.adapters = adapters
   110	    app.state.conn_factory = conn_factory
   111	
   112	    @app.exception_handler(PlannerError)
   113	    async def handle_planner_error(request: Request, exc: PlannerError) -> JSONResponse:
   114	        return JSONResponse(status_code=http_status_for(exc.code), content=exc.to_payload())
   115	
   116	    for domain_router in (
   117	        tickets_router,
   118	        sprints_router,
   119	        days_router,
   120	        dispatch_router,
   121	        seed_router,
   122	        chat_router,
   123	    ):
   124	        app.include_router(domain_router, prefix="/api")
   125	
   126	    @app.get("/api/meta")
   127	    async def meta() -> dict[str, Any]:
   128	        return {
   129	            "ui_debounce_ms": config.ui_debounce_ms,
   130	            "ws_poll_ms": config.ws_poll_ms,
   131	            "test_mode": config.test_mode,
   132	        }
   133	
   134	    @app.websocket("/api/events")
   135	    async def events_ws(websocket: WebSocket, since: int = 0) -> None:
   136	        await tail_events(
   137	            websocket, since, conn_factory, config.ws_poll_ms, config.events_read_limit
   138	        )
   139	
   140	    @app.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
   141	    async def index() -> str:
   142	        return _SHELL
   143	
   144	    if config.test_mode:
   145	        app.include_router(
   146	            build_test_router(config, clock, adapters, conn_factory), prefix="/api"
   147	        )
   148	
   149	    app.mount("/assets", StaticFiles(directory="assets"), name="assets")
   150	    return app

     1	"""Real adapters: subprocess spawn of the hermes worker (§7.4), non-interactive
     2	hermes boundary/replan invocation (best-effort — live use is §18.4), and the guarded
     3	tui_gateway chat gateway (§11). Never imported into a test's execution path except

     8	import importlib
     9	import json
    10	import os
    11	import subprocess
    12	from dataclasses import asdict
    13	from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, Final

    15	from planner.chat.contracts import ChatSendResult, GatewayStatus
    16	from planner.core.adapters.base import (
    17	    BoundaryInputs,

    21	)

    27	    from planner.core.config import Config

    29	BOUNDARY_SKILL: Final = "planning-boundary"     # §17 skill; §13 names no config key (RD-3)
    30	_GATEWAY_MODULE: Final = "tui_gateway.ws"
    31	
    32	
    33	def _parse_json_object(text: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    34	    """Slice the first ``{`` to the last ``}`` inclusive and parse. All failures
    35	    raise; the caller event-logs them (RD-16)."""
    36	    start = text.find("{")
    37	    end = text.rfind("}")
    38	    if start == -1 or end == -1 or end < start:
    39	        raise ValueError("no JSON object in hermes output")
    40	    parsed = json.loads(text[start : end + 1])
    41	    if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
    42	        raise ValueError("hermes output is not a JSON object")
    43	    result: dict[str, Any] = parsed
    44	    return result
    45	
    46	
    47	class RealSpawnAdapter:
    48	    def __init__(self, config: Config) -> None:
    49	        self._config = config
    50	
    51	    def spawn(self, request: SpawnRequest) -> SpawnResult:
    52	        env = dict(os.environ)
    53	        env["PLAN_SERVER_URL"] = request.server_url
    54	        env["PLAN_TICKET_ID"] = request.ticket_id
    55	        env["PLAN_RUN_ID"] = request.run_id
    56	        env["PLAN_CLAIM"] = request.claim
    57	        command = [
    58	            request.hermes_bin,
    59	            "-p",
    60	            request.profile,
    61	            "--skills",
    62	            request.skill,
    63	            "chat",
    64	            "-q",
    65	            f"work planning ticket {request.ticket_id}",
    66	        ]
    67	        try:
    68	            with open(request.log_path, "wb") as log_file:  # parent fd closed after Popen
    69	                process = subprocess.Popen(
    70	                    command,
    71	                    start_new_session=True,
    72	                    stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
    73	                    stdout=log_file,
    74	                    stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
    75	                    env=env,
    76	                )
    77	        except OSError as exc:
    78	            return SpawnResult(ok=False, error=str(exc))
    79	        return SpawnResult(ok=True, pid=process.pid)
    80	
    81	
    82	class RealBoundaryAdapter:
    83	    def __init__(self, config: Config) -> None:
    84	        self._config = config
    85	
    86	    def _invoke(self, prompt: str) -> str:
    87	        """Run hermes non-interactively. The CALLER owns the timeout (no subprocess
    88	        timeout here); a non-zero exit raises."""
    89	        result = subprocess.run(
    90	            [
    91	                self._config.hermes_bin,
    92	                "-p",
    93	                self._config.hermes_profile,
    94	                "--skills",
    95	                BOUNDARY_SKILL,
    96	                "chat",
    97	                "-q",
    98	                prompt,
    99	            ],
   100	            capture_output=True,
   101	            text=True,
   102	            check=False,
   103	        )
   104	        if result.returncode != 0:
   105	            tail = result.stderr.strip()[-500:]
   106	            raise RuntimeError(f"hermes exited {result.returncode}: {tail}")
   107	        return result.stdout
   108	
   109	    def judgment(self, inputs: BoundaryInputs) -> BoundaryJudgment:
   110	        prompt = (
   111	            "You are the planning boundary judgment. Reply with exactly one JSON "
   112	            'object {"brief_markdown": string, "plan_tree": {"root": {"focus", '
   113	            '"status"}, "children": [...]}} and nothing else. Inputs: '
   114	            + json.dumps(asdict(inputs))
   115	        )
   116	        payload = _parse_json_object(self._invoke(prompt))
   117	        return BoundaryJudgment(
   118	            brief_markdown=str(payload["brief_markdown"]),
   119	            plan_tree=tree_from_dict(payload["plan_tree"]),
   120	        )
   121	
   122	    def replan_root(self, day_id: str, inputs: BoundaryInputs) -> PlanTree:
   123	        prompt = (
   124	            f"Replan the full day plan for {day_id}. Reply with exactly one JSON "
   125	            'object {"plan_tree": {"root": {"focus", "status"}, "children": [...]}} '
   126	            "and nothing else. Inputs: " + json.dumps(asdict(inputs))
   127	        )
   128	        payload = _parse_json_object(self._invoke(prompt))
   129	        return tree_from_dict(payload["plan_tree"])
   130	
   131	    def replan_child(self, day_id: str, child: PlanNode, inputs: BoundaryInputs) -> PlanNode:
   132	        prompt = (
   133	            f"Replan a single plan child for {day_id}. Reply with exactly one JSON "
   134	            'object {"child": {"ticket_id": string|null, "note": string}} and nothing '
   135	            "else. Child: "
   136	            + json.dumps(
   137	                {"ticket_id": child.ticket_id, "note": child.note, "position": child.position}
   138	            )
   139	            + " Inputs: "
   140	            + json.dumps(asdict(inputs))
   141	        )
   142	        payload = _parse_json_object(self._invoke(prompt))
   143	        node = payload["child"]
   144	        ticket_id = node["ticket_id"]
   145	        return PlanNode(
   146	            ticket_id=str(ticket_id) if ticket_id is not None else None,
   147	            note=str(node["note"]),
   148	            status=NodeStatus.proposed,
   149	            position=child.position,
   150	        )
   151	
   152	
   153	class RealGatewayAdapter:
   154	    def __init__(self, config: Config) -> None:
   155	        self._config = config
   156	
   157	    def status(self) -> GatewayStatus:
   158	        try:
   159	            importlib.import_module(_GATEWAY_MODULE)
   160	        except ImportError as exc:
   161	            return GatewayStatus(available=False, detail=str(exc))
   162	        return GatewayStatus(available=True)
   163	
   164	    def send(self, session_key: str | None, entity_id: str, text: str) -> ChatSendResult:
   165	        try:
   166	            module = importlib.import_module(_GATEWAY_MODULE)
   167	        except ImportError as exc:
   168	            raise PlannerError(
   169	                ErrorCode.gateway_offline, "chat gateway unavailable", {"detail": str(exc)}
   170	            ) from exc
   171	        try:
   172	            raw = module.send_message(
   173	                session_key=session_key, entity_id=entity_id, text=text
   174	            )
   175	            return ChatSendResult(
   176	                reply_text=str(raw["reply_text"]), session_key=str(raw["session_key"])
   177	            )
   178	        except Exception as exc:
   179	            raise PlannerError(
   180	                ErrorCode.gateway_offline, "chat gateway send failed", {"detail": str(exc)}
   181	            ) from exc

/bin/zsh -lc 'nl -ba src/planner/days/logic/tree.py' in /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Plan-tree transforms (§6.3) as pure functions ``PlanTree → (new_tree, effects)``,
     2	plus JSON serialization of the tree for the ``days.plan`` column and for event
     3	payloads that carry ``old_tree``. No side effects; the data layer persists the
     4	returned tree and applies the effects. Stdlib + planner contract modules only."""
     5	
     6	from __future__ import annotations
     7	
     8	from dataclasses import replace
     9	
    10	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind, JsonDict
    11	from planner.days.contracts import NodeStatus, PlanNode, PlanRoot, PlanTree
    12	from planner.days.logic.effects import (
    13	    AddTicketToDay,
    14	    Effect,
    15	    EmitEvent,
    16	    ReplanChild,
    17	    ReplanRoot,
    18	)
    19	
    20	# A node address in the plan tree: the literal ``"root"`` or a child ``position``.
    21	# Mirrors the plan_node_accepted / plan_node_invalidated payload contract, whose
    22	# ``{node}`` value is ``"root" | <child position>``.
    23	NodeRef = str | int
    24	
    25	
    26	def tree_to_dict(tree: PlanTree) -> JsonDict:
    27	    """{root:{focus,status}, children:[{ticket_id,note,status,position}, ...]}.
    28	
    29	    NodeStatus serialized as its str value; child order preserved.
    30	    """
    31	    return {
    32	        "root": {"focus": tree.root.focus, "status": tree.root.status.value},
    33	        "children": [
    34	            {
    35	                "ticket_id": child.ticket_id,
    36	                "note": child.note,
    37	                "status": child.status.value,
    38	                "position": child.position,
    39	            }
    40	            for child in tree.children
    41	        ],
    42	    }
    43	
    44	
    45	def tree_from_dict(data: JsonDict) -> PlanTree:
    46	    """Inverse of ``tree_to_dict``. status parsed via ``NodeStatus(...)``."""
    47	    root = data["root"]
    48	    children = [
    49	        PlanNode(
    50	            ticket_id=child["ticket_id"],
    51	            note=child["note"],
    52	            status=NodeStatus(child["status"]),
    53	            position=child["position"],
    54	        )
    55	        for child in data["children"]
    56	    ]
    57	    return PlanTree(
    58	        root=PlanRoot(focus=root["focus"], status=NodeStatus(root["status"])),
    59	        children=children,
    60	    )
    61	
    62	
    63	def as_proposed(tree: PlanTree) -> PlanTree:
    64	    """Return a copy with root and every child status=proposed. The boundary
    65	    stores the judgment tree as a PROPOSED plan (§6.2)."""
    66	    return PlanTree(
    67	        root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.proposed),
    68	        children=[replace(child, status=NodeStatus.proposed) for child in tree.children],
    69	    )
    70	
    71	
    72	def accept_node(tree: PlanTree, ref: NodeRef) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
    73	    """§6.3: accepting a node sets it accepted; accepting root does NOT cascade.
    74	
    75	    A child with a ticket_id also yields an AddTicketToDay effect (appended at
    76	    end if absent). Effect order: EmitEvent(plan_node_accepted) then (for a child
    77	    with a ticket_id) AddTicketToDay.
    78	    """
    79	    if ref == "root":
    80	        new_tree = PlanTree(
    81	            root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.accepted),
    82	            children=[replace(child) for child in tree.children],
    83	        )
    84	        return new_tree, [EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_accepted, {"node": "root"})]
    85	
    86	    accepted: PlanNode | None = None
    87	    children: list[PlanNode] = []
    88	    for child in tree.children:
    89	        if child.position == ref:
    90	            accepted = replace(child, status=NodeStatus.accepted)
    91	            children.append(accepted)
    92	        else:
    93	            children.append(replace(child))
    94	    new_tree = PlanTree(root=replace(tree.root), children=children)
    95	    effects: list[Effect] = [EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_accepted, {"node": ref})]
    96	    if accepted is not None and accepted.ticket_id:
    97	        effects.append(AddTicketToDay(accepted.ticket_id))
    98	    return new_tree, effects
    99	
   100	
   101	def accept_all(tree: PlanTree) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
   102	    """§6.3 accept-all: root + every child accepted. Effects =
   103	    [EmitEvent(plan_accepted_all, {})] then AddTicketToDay(child.ticket_id) for
   104	    each child with a ticket_id, in child position order. Idempotent add is the
   105	    data layer's job (a17)."""
   106	    new_tree = PlanTree(
   107	        root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.accepted),
   108	        children=[replace(child, status=NodeStatus.accepted) for child in tree.children],
   109	    )
   110	    effects: list[Effect] = [EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_accepted_all, {})]
   111	    for child in tree.children:
   112	        if child.ticket_id:
   113	            effects.append(AddTicketToDay(child.ticket_id))
   114	    return new_tree, effects
   115	
   116	
   117	def invalidate_root(tree: PlanTree) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
   118	    """§6.3: root + EVERY child → invalidated; request exactly one root replan."""
   119	    new_tree = PlanTree(
   120	        root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.invalidated),
   121	        children=[replace(child, status=NodeStatus.invalidated) for child in tree.children],
   122	    )
   123	    effects: list[Effect] = [
   124	        EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_invalidated, {"node": "root"}),
   125	        ReplanRoot(),
   126	    ]
   127	    return new_tree, effects
   128	
   129	
   130	def invalidate_child(tree: PlanTree, position: int) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
   131	    """§6.3: ONLY the child at ``position`` → invalidated; root and every other
   132	    child keep their prior status. Requests exactly one child replan."""
   133	    children: list[PlanNode] = []
   134	    for child in tree.children:
   135	        if child.position == position:
   136	            children.append(replace(child, status=NodeStatus.invalidated))
   137	        else:
   138	            children.append(replace(child))
   139	    new_tree = PlanTree(root=replace(tree.root), children=children)
   140	    effects: list[Effect] = [
   141	        EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_invalidated, {"node": position}),
   142	        ReplanChild(position),
   143	    ]
   144	    return new_tree, effects
   145	
   146	
   147	def reject_all(tree: PlanTree) -> tuple[None, list[Effect]]:
   148	    """§6.3 reject-all: plan cleared. New tree is None (the data layer writes
   149	    days.plan = NULL). The old tree is preserved in the event payload."""
   150	    effects: list[Effect] = [
   151	        EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_rejected, {"old_tree": tree_to_dict(tree)})
   152	    ]
   153	    return None, effects

/bin/zsh -lc 'nl -ba src/planner/days/logic/carryover.py' in /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""The boundary deterministic pass (§6.2) as pure functions over plain projected
     2	row data. Produce the ``BoundaryInputs`` digest lists: carryover, overdue,
     3	approvals — plus the yesterday done/not-done split for ``day_closed``. Zero DB
     4	access; the boundary layer does the SQL and hands rows in. Stdlib + planner
     5	contract modules only."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	from typing import Any
    10	
    11	from planner.sprints.contracts import ItemStatus
    12	from planner.tickets.contracts import GATING_FIELD, TicketState
    13	
    14	# §6.2.2 — carryover excludes tickets that are already terminal.
    15	_CARRYOVER_EXCLUDE = {TicketState.done, TicketState.dropped}
    16	# Overdue excludes items that are done or already deferred to next sprint.
    17	_ITEM_CLOSED = {ItemStatus.done, ItemStatus.deferred_next_sprint}
    18	
    19	
    20	def carryover_candidates(yesterday_tickets: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    21	    """§6.2 step 2: yesterday's day-tickets whose ticket state is NOT done/dropped.
    22	
    23	    Input rows carry {id, title, state, priority} (ticket joined to its day-ticket,
    24	    in day-ticket position order). Output digest keeps the same four keys. StrEnum
    25	    members compare equal to their string values, so plain state strings work.
    26	    """
    27	    return [
    28	        {
    29	            "id": row["id"],
    30	            "title": row["title"],
    31	            "state": row["state"],
    32	            "priority": row["priority"],
    33	        }
    34	        for row in yesterday_tickets
    35	        if row["state"] not in _CARRYOVER_EXCLUDE
    36	    ]
    37	
    38	
    39	def day_ticket_counts(yesterday_tickets: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> tuple[int, int]:
    40	    """§6.2 step 4: (done_count, not_done_count) over yesterday's day-tickets.
    41	
    42	    done_count = rows with state == done; not_done_count = the rest (dropped and
    43	    every unfinished state). The two sum to len(rows).
    44	    """
    45	    done_count = sum(1 for row in yesterday_tickets if row["state"] == TicketState.done)
    46	    return done_count, len(yesterday_tickets) - done_count
    47	
    48	
    49	def overdue_list(
    50	    tickets: list[dict[str, Any]], items: list[dict[str, Any]], today_iso: str
    51	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    52	    """§6.2 step 3 (overdue). A ticket is overdue iff deadline is not None and
    53	    deadline < today_iso (strict; due-today is NOT overdue) and state not in
    54	    {done, dropped}. An item is overdue iff deadline < today_iso and status not
    55	    in {done, deferred_next_sprint}. Uniform digest {id, title, state, priority}
    56	    (for items, state = the item status). ISO date strings compare lexically.
    57	    Tickets first, then items."""
    58	    result: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    59	    for row in tickets:
    60	        deadline = row["deadline"]
    61	        if deadline is not None and deadline < today_iso and row["state"] not in _CARRYOVER_EXCLUDE:
    62	            result.append(
    63	                {
    64	                    "id": row["id"],
    65	                    "title": row["title"],
    66	                    "state": row["state"],
    67	                    "priority": row["priority"],
    68	                }
    69	            )
    70	    for row in items:
    71	        deadline = row["deadline"]
    72	        if deadline is not None and deadline < today_iso and row["status"] not in _ITEM_CLOSED:
    73	            result.append(
    74	                {
    75	                    "id": row["id"],
    76	                    "title": row["title"],
    77	                    "state": row["status"],
    78	                    "priority": row["priority"],
    79	                }
    80	            )
    81	    return result
    82	
    83	
    84	def approvals_digest(
    85	    tickets: list[dict[str, Any]], items: list[dict[str, Any]]
    86	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    87	    """§6.2 step 3 / §4.5 approval-queue digest. Three sources, oldest-pending first:
    88	
    89	    * a ticket with a non-null proposal on its current gating field
    90	      (GATING_FIELD[state]); kind = the gating field name (e.g. "plan"),
    91	      waiting_since = proposal["created_at"];
    92	    * a ticket in ``needs_review`` (§4.5); kind = "review", waiting_since =
    93	      the ticket's updated_at (DB-internal proxy for review entry time);
    94	    * a sprint item with a non-null status_proposal; kind = "status",
    95	      waiting_since = proposal["created_at"].
    96	
    97	    Digest per base.py: {entity_id, kind, waiting_since}. Ticket rows carry
    98	    {id, state, fields(dict), updated_at}; item rows carry {id, status_proposal}.
    99	    """
   100	    digest: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   101	    for row in tickets:
   102	        state = row["state"]
   103	        if state == TicketState.needs_review:
   104	            digest.append(
   105	                {"entity_id": row["id"], "kind": "review", "waiting_since": row["updated_at"]}
   106	            )
   107	            continue
   108	        gating = GATING_FIELD.get(TicketState(state))
   109	        if gating is None:
   110	            continue
   111	        slot = row["fields"].get(gating.value)
   112	        if slot is None:
   113	            continue
   114	        proposal = slot.get("proposal")
   115	        if proposal is None:
   116	            continue
   117	        digest.append(
   118	            {"entity_id": row["id"], "kind": gating.value, "waiting_since": proposal["created_at"]}
   119	        )
   120	    for row in items:
   121	        proposal = row["status_proposal"]
   122	        if proposal is None:
   123	            continue
   124	        digest.append(
   125	            {"entity_id": row["id"], "kind": "status", "waiting_since": proposal["created_at"]}
   126	        )
   127	    digest.sort(key=lambda entry: entry["waiting_since"])
   128	    return digest

    57	def _parse_bool(raw: object, key: str) -> bool:
    58	    if isinstance(raw, bool):
    59	        return raw
    60	    text = str(raw).strip().lower()
    61	    if text in _TRUE:
    62	        return True
    63	    if text in _FALSE:
    64	        return False
    65	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid boolean for {key}: {raw!r}")
    66	
    67	
    68	def _parse_int(raw: object, key: str) -> int:
    69	    if isinstance(raw, bool):
    70	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid integer for {key}: {raw!r}")
    71	    if isinstance(raw, int):
    72	        return raw
    73	    if isinstance(raw, str):
    74	        try:
    75	            return int(raw.strip())
    76	        except ValueError as exc:
    77	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid integer for {key}: {raw!r}") from exc
    78	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid integer for {key}: {raw!r}")
    79	
    80	
    81	def _read_yaml(path: str | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    82	    resolved = Path(path) if path is not None else Path(DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH)
    83	    if not resolved.exists():
    84	        return {}
    85	    loaded = yaml.safe_load(resolved.read_text())
    86	    if loaded is None:
    87	        return {}
    88	    if not isinstance(loaded, dict):
    89	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"config root must be a mapping: {resolved}")
    90	    return loaded

    96	    value: object = default

    99	    if env_var in env:

   101	    return str(value)

     1	"""Seed route (§9): POST /api/seed runs a migration and returns a MigrationReport."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import sqlite3
     6	from collections.abc import Callable
     7	from dataclasses import asdict
     8	from pathlib import Path
     9	from typing import Any
    10	
    11	from fastapi import APIRouter, Request
    12	
    13	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    14	from planner.seed.contracts import MigrationReport
    15	from planner.seed.demo import seed_demo
    16	from planner.seed.importer import seed_from_source
    17	
    18	router = APIRouter()
    19	
    20	
    21	def _demo_report(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> MigrationReport:
    22	    def count(sql: str) -> int:
    23	        return int(conn.execute(sql).fetchone()[0])
    24	
    25	    return MigrationReport(
    26	        sprints=count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sprints"),
    27	        sprint_items=count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sprint_items WHERE sprint_id IS NOT NULL"),
    28	        deferred_items=count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sprint_items WHERE sprint_id IS NULL"),
    29	        tickets=count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tickets"),
    30	        ideas=count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ideas"),
    31	        links=count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM links WHERE kind = 'belongs_to'"),
    32	        duplicates_skipped=0,
    33	        skipped=[],
    34	    )
    35	
    36	
    37	@router.post("/seed")
    38	async def seed(body: dict[str, Any], request: Request) -> dict[str, Any]:
    39	    source_dir = body.get("source_dir")
    40	    demo = bool(body.get("demo", False))
    41	    has_source = source_dir is not None
    42	    if has_source == demo:  # both present, or neither
    43	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "provide exactly one of source_dir or demo")
    44	    conn_factory: Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection] = request.app.state.conn_factory
    45	    conn = conn_factory()
    46	    try:
    47	        if demo:
    48	            seed_demo(conn)  # raises db_not_empty on a non-empty database
    49	            report = _demo_report(conn)
    50	        else:
    51	            if not isinstance(source_dir, str) or not Path(source_dir).is_dir():
    52	                raise PlannerError(
    53	                    ErrorCode.validation, "source_dir must be an existing directory"
    54	                )
    55	            report = seed_from_source(conn, source_dir)
    56	    finally:
    57	        conn.close()
    58	    return asdict(report)

DOCS.md:40:- **Stage 4 — the server (done):** the rules now answer the door. One local process serves a JSON interface for every action in the system, and it enforces who may do what: requests from a working agent must carry a valid claim on the ticket they're touching (a stale or borrowed claim is refused, and the refusal names what was wrong without ever revealing the live claim itself); the actions reserved for the human — approving, granting, unblocking, dropping, freezing, deciding the day plan — refuse agents entirely. A live feed announces every change so screens can refresh themselves; the feed is only a doorbell, never the data. The dispatcher and the morning boundary job now actually run on their schedules (in testing they run only when explicitly ticked, so tests are exact). The command-line tool agents use is complete: every verb talks to the server, returns clean machine-readable output, and deliberately contains no approval powers at all. Four review findings worth naming, all caught by the independent reviewer and fixed: a path that leaked the live claim token into an error detail; a check that ran validation before verifying the claim; a number that could sneak into a date field untyped; and an environment value that could trick the tool into reading the wrong input.

     1	"""The day data layer. Materializes a day on first read/write (no "missing day"
     2	state), owns the ordered day-ticket list (contiguous positions from 0), plan JSON
     3	storage, brief/notes writes, and applies the logic layer's plan-tree effects.
     4	Every state transition here appends exactly one canonical event. No FastAPI/
     5	pydantic; times come in as unix-second ints from the caller's clock."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	import json
    10	import sqlite3
    11	
    12	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind
    13	from planner.core.events import append_event
    14	from planner.days.contracts import Day, DayTicket, PlanTree
    15	from planner.days.logic.effects import (
    16	    AddTicketToDay,
    17	    Effect,
    18	    EmitEvent,
    19	    ReplanChild,
    20	    ReplanRequest,
    21	    ReplanRoot,
    22	)
    23	from planner.days.logic.tree import tree_from_dict, tree_to_dict
    24	
    25	
    26	def materialize_day(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, now_unix: int) -> None:
    27	    """§3.4: create the day row if absent (brief='', notes='', plan=NULL,
    28	    chat_session_key=NULL, created_at=updated_at=now_unix) and append a
    29	    day_created event. Idempotent: a present day → no write, no event."""
    30	    if conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM days WHERE id = ?", (day_id,)).fetchone() is not None:
    31	        return
    32	    conn.execute(
    33	        "INSERT INTO days (id, brief, notes, plan, chat_session_key, created_at, updated_at) "
    34	        "VALUES (?, '', '', NULL, NULL, ?, ?)",
    35	        (day_id, now_unix, now_unix),
    36	    )
    37	    append_event(conn, day_id, EventKind.day_created, {}, now_unix)
    38	
    39	
    40	def read_day(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, now_unix: int) -> Day:
    41	    """§3.4 'reading a nonexistent day materializes it empty'. Materializes, then
    42	    SELECTs the row and builds a Day (plan parsed via tree_from_dict when set)."""
    43	    materialize_day(conn, day_id, now_unix)
    44	    row = conn.execute(
    45	        "SELECT id, brief, notes, plan, chat_session_key, created_at, updated_at "
    46	        "FROM days WHERE id = ?",
    47	        (day_id,),
    48	    ).fetchone()
    49	    assert row is not None
    50	    plan_json = row["plan"]
    51	    return Day(
    52	        id=row["id"],
    53	        brief=row["brief"],
    54	        notes=row["notes"],
    55	        plan=tree_from_dict(json.loads(plan_json)) if plan_json is not None else None,
    56	        chat_session_key=row["chat_session_key"],
    57	        created_at=row["created_at"],
    58	        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
    59	    )
    60	
    61	
    62	def list_day_tickets(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str) -> list[DayTicket]:
    63	    """day_tickets for the day, ordered by position (contiguous from 0)."""
    64	    rows = conn.execute(
    65	        "SELECT day_id, ticket_id, position FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ? ORDER BY position",
    66	        (day_id,),
    67	    ).fetchall()
    68	    return [
    69	        DayTicket(day_id=row["day_id"], ticket_id=row["ticket_id"], position=row["position"])
    70	        for row in rows
    71	    ]
    72	
    73	
    74	def load_plan(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str) -> PlanTree | None:
    75	    """Parse days.plan JSON → PlanTree, or None (absent day or NULL plan)."""
    76	    row = conn.execute("SELECT plan FROM days WHERE id = ?", (day_id,)).fetchone()
    77	    if row is None or row["plan"] is None:
    78	        return None
    79	    return tree_from_dict(json.loads(row["plan"]))
    80	
    81	
    82	def store_plan(
    83	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, tree: PlanTree | None, now_unix: int
    84	) -> None:
    85	    """Materialize, then write days.plan = tree_to_dict(tree) JSON (or NULL) and
    86	    bump updated_at. No event here (callers emit the specific plan_* event)."""
    87	    materialize_day(conn, day_id, now_unix)
    88	    plan_json = json.dumps(tree_to_dict(tree)) if tree is not None else None
    89	    conn.execute(
    90	        "UPDATE days SET plan = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
    91	        (plan_json, now_unix, day_id),
    92	    )
    93	
    94	
    95	def store_judgment(
    96	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, brief: str, tree: PlanTree, now_unix: int
    97	) -> None:
    98	    """Boundary success path: write days.brief + days.plan (serialized) +
    99	    updated_at in one UPDATE. No day_updated event (that kind is reserved for
   100	    manual edits); the boundary emits plan_proposed separately."""
   101	    conn.execute(
   102	        "UPDATE days SET brief = ?, plan = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   103	        (brief, json.dumps(tree_to_dict(tree)), now_unix, day_id),
   104	    )
   105	
   106	
   107	def add_day_ticket(
   108	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, ticket_id: str, now_unix: int, cause: str = "manual"
   109	) -> bool:
   110	    """§3.4: append at end with the next contiguous position (= current count).
   111	    Idempotent: if (day_id, ticket_id) already present → return False, no event.
   112	    On add → INSERT, append day_ticket_added {ticket_id, position, cause}, bump
   113	    updated_at, return True. Materializes the day first (write path)."""
   114	    materialize_day(conn, day_id, now_unix)
   115	    present = conn.execute(
   116	        "SELECT 1 FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ? AND ticket_id = ?",
   117	        (day_id, ticket_id),
   118	    ).fetchone()
   119	    if present is not None:
   120	        return False
   121	    position = conn.execute(
   122	        "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ?", (day_id,)
   123	    ).fetchone()["n"]
   124	    conn.execute(
   125	        "INSERT INTO day_tickets (day_id, ticket_id, position) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
   126	        (day_id, ticket_id, position),
   127	    )
   128	    append_event(
   129	        conn,
   130	        day_id,
   131	        EventKind.day_ticket_added,
   132	        {"ticket_id": ticket_id, "position": position, "cause": cause},
   133	        now_unix,
   134	    )
   135	    conn.execute("UPDATE days SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (now_unix, day_id))
   136	    return True
   137	
   138	
   139	def remove_day_ticket(
   140	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, ticket_id: str, now_unix: int
   141	) -> None:
   142	    """§3.4: delete the association only (ticket state untouched). If nothing was
   143	    deleted (rowcount 0) → no-op, no event, no re-pack. Else re-pack remaining
   144	    positions to 0..n-1 in existing position order, append day_ticket_removed
   145	    {ticket_id}, bump updated_at."""
   146	    cursor = conn.execute(
   147	        "DELETE FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ? AND ticket_id = ?",
   148	        (day_id, ticket_id),
   149	    )
   150	    if cursor.rowcount == 0:
   151	        return
   152	    survivors = conn.execute(
   153	        "SELECT ticket_id FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ? ORDER BY position",
   154	        (day_id,),
   155	    ).fetchall()
   156	    for new_position, row in enumerate(survivors):
   157	        conn.execute(
   158	            "UPDATE day_tickets SET position = ? WHERE day_id = ? AND ticket_id = ?",
   159	            (new_position, day_id, row["ticket_id"]),
   160	        )
   161	    append_event(conn, day_id, EventKind.day_ticket_removed, {"ticket_id": ticket_id}, now_unix)
   162	    conn.execute("UPDATE days SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (now_unix, day_id))
   163	
   164	
   165	def set_brief(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, brief: str, now_unix: int) -> None:
   166	    """Manual brief edit: materialize, UPDATE brief + updated_at, append
   167	    day_updated {field: "brief"}."""
   168	    materialize_day(conn, day_id, now_unix)
   169	    conn.execute(
   170	        "UPDATE days SET brief = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (brief, now_unix, day_id)
   171	    )
   172	    append_event(conn, day_id, EventKind.day_updated, {"field": "brief"}, now_unix)
   173	
   174	
   175	def set_notes(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, notes: str, now_unix: int) -> None:
   176	    """Manual notes edit: materialize, UPDATE notes + updated_at, append
   177	    day_updated {field: "notes"}."""
   178	    materialize_day(conn, day_id, now_unix)
   179	    conn.execute(
   180	        "UPDATE days SET notes = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (notes, now_unix, day_id)
   181	    )
   182	    append_event(conn, day_id, EventKind.day_updated, {"field": "notes"}, now_unix)
   183	
   184	
   185	def apply_plan_effects(
   186	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   187	    day_id: str,
   188	    new_tree: PlanTree | None,
   189	    effects: list[Effect],
   190	    now_unix: int,
   191	) -> list[ReplanRequest]:
   192	    """Persist the transform result and apply its effects. (1) store_plan writes
   193	    days.plan (or NULL for reject-all). (2) For each effect in order:
   194	    AddTicketToDay → add_day_ticket(cause="plan_accept") (idempotent);
   195	    EmitEvent → append_event; ReplanRoot/ReplanChild → collected. (3) Return the
   196	    collected replan requests for the stage-4 runtime to serialize + execute (R5).
   197	    At stage 3 no adapter is called here."""
   198	    store_plan(conn, day_id, new_tree, now_unix)
   199	    replans: list[ReplanRequest] = []
   200	    for effect in effects:
   201	        if isinstance(effect, AddTicketToDay):
   202	            add_day_ticket(conn, day_id, effect.ticket_id, now_unix, cause="plan_accept")
   203	        elif isinstance(effect, EmitEvent):
   204	            append_event(conn, day_id, effect.kind, effect.payload, now_unix)
   205	        elif isinstance(effect, (ReplanRoot, ReplanChild)):
   206	            replans.append(effect)
   207	    return replans

orchestration/tickets/T21-dogfood-skills/codex-plan-review-raw.txt:586:314:35. Dogfood Level A: `scripts/dogfood_cli.py` drives the full workflow against a test server seeded with `plan seed --demo` — agent actions through the real CLI, human actions (accepts with grant pairs, needs_review approve, day-plan decisions) through the HTTP API, since the CLI deliberately has no human verbs: create a ticket; propose and accept through every gate — every accept carrying its grant pair — including one edit-accept and one superseded proposal; write and overwrite the recap; assign to the day and remove; create a blocking link and confirm dispatch-ineligibility, then complete the blocker and confirm eligibility; claim via the test dispatcher tick; propose result with claim env; approve from needs_review to done. The script asserts each step's resulting state via `--json`/response output and exits non-zero on the first mismatch.

     1	"""Write admission: who may write what, when. The §4.3 agent-proposal matrix,
     2	the human-only gate (§7.6), recap/sprint/title/deadline/body validators (§3.3).
     3	Pure: contracts/errors + machine only; raises PlannerError as the rejection."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from datetime import date
     8	from typing import Final
     9	
    10	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    11	from planner.tickets.contracts import AtCap, FieldName, TicketState
    12	from planner.tickets.logic import machine
    13	
    14	HUMAN_ACTOR: Final[str] = "human"
    15	
    16	
    17	def is_human(actor: str) -> bool:
    18	    return actor == HUMAN_ACTOR
    19	
    20	
    21	def require_human(actor: str, action: str) -> None:
    22	    if actor != HUMAN_ACTOR:
    23	        raise PlannerError(
    24	            ErrorCode.agent_forbidden,
    25	            f"{action} is a human-only action",
    26	            {"action": action, "actor": actor},
    27	        )
    28	
    29	
    30	def check_agent_proposal(
    31	    state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState, at_cap: AtCap, field: FieldName
    32	) -> None:
    33	    if machine.is_terminal(state):
    34	        raise PlannerError(
    35	            ErrorCode.validation, "no proposals on a terminal ticket", {"state": state.value}
    36	        )
    37	    gating = machine.gating_field(state)
    38	    if not machine.at_or_beyond_ceiling(state, ceiling):
    39	        return
    40	    if at_cap is AtCap.stop:
    41	        raise PlannerError(
    42	            ErrorCode.at_cap_stop,
    43	            "ticket is at its ceiling with at_cap=stop",
    44	            {
    45	                "gating_field": gating.value if gating is not None else None,
    46	                "state": state.value,
    47	                "ceiling": ceiling.value,
    48	                "at_cap": "stop",
    49	            },
    50	        )
    51	    if field is not gating:
    52	        raise PlannerError(
    53	            ErrorCode.validation,
    54	            "at the ceiling agents may propose only the current gating field",
    55	            {
    56	                "field": field.value,
    57	                "gating_field": gating.value if gating is not None else None,
    58	                "state": state.value,
    59	            },
    60	        )
    61	
    62	
    63	def check_recap_writable(state: TicketState) -> None:
    64	    if state is TicketState.needs_success or state is TicketState.dropped:
    65	        raise PlannerError(
    66	            ErrorCode.recap_too_early,
    67	            "recap is writable only past needs_success",
    68	            {"state": state.value},
    69	        )
    70	
    71	
    72	def check_sprint_assignable(ticket_id: str, sprint_item_id: str | None) -> None:
    73	    if sprint_item_id is not None:
    74	        raise PlannerError(
    75	            ErrorCode.sprint_derived,
    76	            "sprint_id is derived from the parent item",
    77	            {"ticket_id": ticket_id, "sprint_item_id": sprint_item_id},
    78	        )
    79	
    80	
    81	def validate_title(title: str, max_chars: int) -> None:
    82	    if not title:
    83	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "title must be non-empty")
    84	    if len(title) > max_chars:
    85	        raise PlannerError(
    86	            ErrorCode.title_too_long,
    87	            f"title exceeds {max_chars} characters",
    88	            {"length": len(title), "max": max_chars},
    89	        )
    90	
    91	
    92	def validate_deadline(deadline: str | None) -> None:
    93	    if deadline is None:
    94	        return
    95	    try:
    96	        date.fromisoformat(deadline)
    97	    except ValueError as exc:
    98	        raise PlannerError(
    99	            ErrorCode.validation, "deadline must be an ISO date", {"deadline": deadline}
   100	        ) from exc
   101	
   102	
   103	def validate_body(body: str, what: str) -> None:
   104	    if not body:
   105	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"{what} must be non-empty")

     1	"""Ticket domain shapes: the state machine order, the four fields, the grant pair,
     2	and the ticket row. Stdlib only; Priority/Project are imported from core (shared
     3	vocabulary), never redeclared."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     8	from enum import StrEnum
     9	from typing import Final, Literal
    10	
    11	from planner.core.contracts import Priority, Project
    12	
    13	
    14	class TicketState(StrEnum):        # §4.1, exact order
    15	    needs_success = "needs_success"
    16	    needs_approach = "needs_approach"
    17	    needs_plan = "needs_plan"
    18	    in_progress = "in_progress"
    19	    needs_review = "needs_review"
    20	    done = "done"
    21	    dropped = "dropped"            # terminal, human-only, outside the linear order
    22	
    23	
    24	# Linear pipeline order (dropped excluded). Index comparison implements "<= ceiling".
    25	# ceiling and next_ceiling values are restricted to members of this tuple (never dropped).
    26	STATE_ORDER: Final[tuple[TicketState, ...]] = (
    27	    TicketState.needs_success, TicketState.needs_approach, TicketState.needs_plan,
    28	    TicketState.in_progress, TicketState.needs_review, TicketState.done,
    29	)
    30	
    31	
    32	class FieldName(StrEnum):          # §4.2 — the exactly-four field keys
    33	    success = "success"
    34	    approach = "approach"
    35	    plan = "plan"
    36	    result = "result"
    37	
    38	
    39	class AtCap(StrEnum):              # §4.3
    40	    stop = "stop"
    41	    propose = "propose"
    42	
    43	
    44	# §4.2 table — gating field per pre-terminal state. needs_review has none (human approve).
    45	GATING_FIELD: Final[dict[TicketState, FieldName]] = {
    46	    TicketState.needs_success: FieldName.success,
    47	    TicketState.needs_approach: FieldName.approach,
    48	    TicketState.needs_plan: FieldName.plan,
    49	    TicketState.in_progress: FieldName.result,
    50	}
    51	
    52	# §4.2 table — accepted proposal advances to. in_progress -> needs_review is the
    53	# default route; the ceiling=done special case (§4.4.5) is resolution-engine logic,
    54	# not a second table entry.
    55	ADVANCE_TARGET: Final[dict[TicketState, TicketState]] = {
    56	    TicketState.needs_success: TicketState.needs_approach,
    57	    TicketState.needs_approach: TicketState.needs_plan,
    58	    TicketState.needs_plan: TicketState.in_progress,
    59	    TicketState.in_progress: TicketState.needs_review,
    60	}
    61	
    62	
    63	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    64	class Proposal:                    # §4.2 proposal slot

    66	    proposed_by: str               # actor string: "agent", run id context, or PLAN_ACTOR
    67	    created_at: int
    68	
    69

    71	class FieldSlot:                   # §4.2 — one of the four field objects
    72	    value: str | None = None       # canonical; resolution engine is the only writer
    73	    proposal: Proposal | None = None
    74	    notes: str | None = None       # free guidance; result.notes = review notes (§4.2)
    75

    80	    approach: FieldSlot = field(default_factory=FieldSlot)

    82	    result: FieldSlot = field(default_factory=FieldSlot)
    83	
    84	
    85	# --- the grant pair (§4.4.7) ---
    86	NO_FURTHER: Final = "none"                     # wire sentinel: ceiling = the newly entered state
    87	NextCeiling = TicketState | Literal["none"]    # valid TicketState values are STATE_ORDER members
    88
