/bin/zsh -lc 'nl -ba src/planner/core/links.py' in /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Link create/remove over the `links` table plus the blocked-derivation (§3.6,
     2	SPEC lines 54–57). Core infrastructure over the DB (like events.py) — sqlite3 lives
     3	here, not in dispatch/logic. Config-free: `now` is always a parameter."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	import sqlite3
     8	from collections import deque
     9	from typing import Final
    10	
    11	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind, LinkKind
    12	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    13	from planner.core.events import append_event
    14	
    15	# blocks/parent_child are two independently-checked transitive relations (§3.6).
    16	_CYCLE_CHECKED: Final[frozenset[LinkKind]] = frozenset(
    17	    {LinkKind.blocks, LinkKind.parent_child}
    18	)
    19	# Endpoint prefix rules (D8): (allowed from-prefixes, allowed to-prefixes); None = any.
    20	_ENDPOINT_RULES: Final[dict[LinkKind, tuple[frozenset[str] | None, frozenset[str] | None]]] = {
    21	    LinkKind.belongs_to: (frozenset({"t"}), frozenset({"si"})),
    22	    LinkKind.parent_child: (frozenset({"t"}), frozenset({"t"})),
    23	    LinkKind.blocks: (frozenset({"t"}), frozenset({"t", "si"})),
    24	    LinkKind.relates: (None, None),
    25	}
    26	
    27	
    28	def _prefix(entity_id: str) -> str:
    29	    """The endpoint kind is the id prefix before the first '_' (t_x -> 't')."""
    30	    return entity_id.split("_", 1)[0]
    31	
    32	
    33	def add_link(
    34	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, from_id: str, to_id: str, kind: LinkKind, now: int
    35	) -> None:
    36	    """Create a link, enforcing §3.6: no self-links, fixed endpoint kinds per link,
    37	    at most one belongs_to per ticket, and no blocks/parent_child transitive cycle."""
    38	    detail = {"from_id": from_id, "to_id": to_id, "kind": kind.value}
    39	    # 1. Self-link (§3.6): the two endpoints must differ. Pure check, outside the txn.
    40	    if from_id == to_id:
    41	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.link_invalid, "self-links are not allowed", detail)
    42	    # 2. Endpoint kinds (D8): the prefix fixes each side. Pure check, outside the txn.
    43	    from_rule, to_rule = _ENDPOINT_RULES[kind]
    44	    if from_rule is not None and _prefix(from_id) not in from_rule:
    45	        raise PlannerError(
    46	            ErrorCode.link_invalid,
    47	            f"{kind.value} from-endpoint must be one of {sorted(from_rule)}",
    48	            detail,
    49	        )
    50	    if to_rule is not None and _prefix(to_id) not in to_rule:
    51	        raise PlannerError(
    52	            ErrorCode.link_invalid,
    53	            f"{kind.value} to-endpoint must be one of {sorted(to_rule)}",
    54	            detail,
    55	        )
    56	    # Steps 3–6 serialize under the write lock (A2): BEGIN IMMEDIATE takes the lock
    57	    # before the reads, so two connections cannot interleave BFS-then-insert to admit
    58	    # a cycle or a second belongs_to. Contention resolves via db.connect's busy_timeout.
    59	    own_txn = not conn.in_transaction
    60	    if own_txn:
    61	        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    62	    try:
    63	        # 3. belongs_to uniqueness (§3.6): at most one per ticket.
    64	        if kind is LinkKind.belongs_to:
    65	            existing = conn.execute(
    66	                "SELECT 1 FROM links WHERE from_id=? AND kind='belongs_to' LIMIT 1",
    67	                (from_id,),
    68	            ).fetchone()
    69	            if existing is not None:
    70	                raise PlannerError(
    71	                    ErrorCode.link_invalid,
    72	                    "ticket already has a belongs_to link",
    73	                    {"from_id": from_id},
    74	                )
    75	        # 4. Transitive cycle check (§3.6, D10): from->to makes a cycle iff `from` is
    76	        # reachable from `to` along same-kind edges. BFS from to_id, visited-guarded.
    77	        if kind in _CYCLE_CHECKED:
    78	            queue: deque[str] = deque([to_id])
    79	            visited: set[str] = {to_id}
    80	            while queue:
    81	                node = queue.popleft()
    82	                if node == from_id:
    83	                    raise PlannerError(
    84	                        ErrorCode.link_cycle,
    85	                        f"link would create a {kind.value} cycle",
    86	                        detail,
    87	                    )
    88	                rows = conn.execute(
    89	                    "SELECT to_id FROM links WHERE from_id=? AND kind=?",
    90	                    (node, kind.value),
    91	                ).fetchall()
    92	                for succ in rows:
    93	                    nxt = str(succ["to_id"])
    94	                    if nxt not in visited:
    95	                        visited.add(nxt)
    96	                        queue.append(nxt)
    97	        # 5. Insert; any IntegrityError (PK dup, partial unique index race, CHECK) is
    98	        # re-raised as link_invalid (D9) — a raw IntegrityError never escapes.
    99	        try:
   100	            conn.execute(
   101	                "INSERT INTO links (from_id, to_id, kind) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
   102	                (from_id, to_id, kind.value),
   103	            )
   104	        except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:
   105	            raise PlannerError(
   106	                ErrorCode.link_invalid,
   107	                "link already exists or violates a link constraint",
   108	                detail,
   109	            ) from exc
   110	        # 6. Event.
   111	        append_event(conn, from_id, EventKind.link_added, detail, now)
   112	    except BaseException:
   113	        if own_txn:
   114	            conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
   115	        raise
   116	    if own_txn:
   117	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
   118	
   119	
   120	def remove_link(
   121	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, from_id: str, to_id: str, kind: LinkKind, now: int
   122	) -> None:
   123	    """Delete a link; a missing link is not_found (D12)."""
   124	    detail = {"from_id": from_id, "to_id": to_id, "kind": kind.value}
   125	    cursor = conn.execute(
   126	        "DELETE FROM links WHERE from_id=? AND to_id=? AND kind=?",
   127	        (from_id, to_id, kind.value),
   128	    )
   129	    if cursor.rowcount == 0:
   130	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "link not found", detail)
   131	    append_event(conn, from_id, EventKind.link_removed, detail, now)
   132	
   133	
   134	def blocked_target_ids(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> set[str]:
   135	    """Ids (tickets or sprint items) currently blocked: targets of a blocks link
   136	    whose source ticket is not done/dropped (§3.6 line 57)."""
   137	    rows = conn.execute(
   138	        "SELECT DISTINCT l.to_id FROM links l "
   139	        "JOIN tickets src ON src.id = l.from_id "
   140	        "WHERE l.kind = 'blocks' AND src.state NOT IN ('done', 'dropped')"
   141	    ).fetchall()
   142	    return {str(row["to_id"]) for row in rows}
   143	
   144	
   145	def is_blocked(conn: sqlite3.Connection, entity_id: str) -> bool:
   146	    """Whether one entity is blocked. The join to tickets makes the rule literal:
   147	    only a ticket source in an open state blocks; a missing/non-ticket source never
   148	    does (D8)."""
   149	    row = conn.execute(
   150	        "SELECT 1 FROM links l "
   151	        "JOIN tickets src ON src.id = l.from_id "
   152	        "WHERE l.kind = 'blocks' AND src.state NOT IN ('done', 'dropped') "
   153	        "AND l.to_id = ? LIMIT 1",
   154	        (entity_id,),
   155	    ).fetchone()
   156	    return row is not None

     1	"""In-memory fakes for the three adapters. Tests use these; they never touch the
     2	OS or the network. Each records its calls and behaves deterministically."""
     3	
     4	from __future__ import annotations
     5	
     6	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7	
     8	from planner.chat.contracts import ChatSendResult, GatewayStatus
     9	from planner.core.adapters.base import (
    10	    BoundaryInputs,
    11	    BoundaryJudgment,
    12	    SpawnRequest,
    13	    SpawnResult,
    14	)
    15	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    16	from planner.days.contracts import NodeStatus, PlanNode, PlanRoot, PlanTree
    17	
    18	_FIRST_FAKE_PID = 90001
    19	
    20	
    21	@dataclass
    22	class FakeSpawnAdapter:
    23	    calls: list[SpawnRequest] = field(default_factory=list)
    24	    results: list[SpawnResult] = field(default_factory=list)  # scripted, popped FIFO
    25	    next_pid: int = _FIRST_FAKE_PID
    26	
    27	    def spawn(self, request: SpawnRequest) -> SpawnResult:
    28	        self.calls.append(request)
    29	        if self.results:
    30	            return self.results.pop(0)
    31	        pid = self.next_pid
    32	        self.next_pid += 1
    33	        return SpawnResult(ok=True, pid=pid)
    34	
    35	
    36	@dataclass
    37	class FakeBoundaryAdapter:
    38	    judgment_result: BoundaryJudgment | None = None
    39	    replan_tree: PlanTree | None = None
    40	    replan_child_node: PlanNode | None = None
    41	    fail: bool = False
    42	    calls: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # method names, in order
    43	
    44	    def judgment(self, inputs: BoundaryInputs) -> BoundaryJudgment:
    45	        self.calls.append("judgment")
    46	        if self.fail:
    47	            raise RuntimeError("fake boundary failure")
    48	        if self.judgment_result is not None:
    49	            return self.judgment_result
    50	        children = [
    51	            PlanNode(
    52	                ticket_id=entry.get("id"),
    53	                note=str(entry.get("title", "")),
    54	                status=NodeStatus.proposed,
    55	                position=index,
    56	            )
    57	            for index, entry in enumerate(inputs.carryover)
    58	        ]
    59	        tree = PlanTree(root=PlanRoot(focus="Fake focus"), children=children)
    60	        return BoundaryJudgment(
    61	            brief_markdown=f"# Brief for {inputs.planning_date}", plan_tree=tree
    62	        )
    63	
    64	    def replan_root(self, day_id: str, inputs: BoundaryInputs) -> PlanTree:
    65	        self.calls.append("replan_root")
    66	        if self.fail:
    67	            raise RuntimeError("fake boundary failure")
    68	        if self.replan_tree is not None:
    69	            return self.replan_tree
    70	        return PlanTree(root=PlanRoot(focus="Fake replanned focus"), children=[])
    71	
    72	    def replan_child(self, day_id: str, child: PlanNode, inputs: BoundaryInputs) -> PlanNode:
    73	        self.calls.append("replan_child")
    74	        if self.fail:
    75	            raise RuntimeError("fake boundary failure")
    76	        if self.replan_child_node is not None:
    77	            return self.replan_child_node
    78	        return PlanNode(
    79	            ticket_id=child.ticket_id,
    80	            note="Fake replanned child",
    81	            status=NodeStatus.proposed,
    82	            position=child.position,
    83	        )
    84	
    85	
    86	@dataclass
    87	class EchoGatewayAdapter:
    88	    calls: list[tuple[str | None, str, str]] = field(default_factory=list)
    89	    next_session: int = 1
    90	
    91	    def status(self) -> GatewayStatus:
    92	        return GatewayStatus(available=True)
    93	
    94	    def send(self, session_key: str | None, entity_id: str, text: str) -> ChatSendResult:
    95	        self.calls.append((session_key, entity_id, text))
    96	        if session_key is None:
    97	            session_key = f"fake-sess-{self.next_session}"
    98	            self.next_session += 1
    99	        return ChatSendResult(reply_text=f"echo: {text}", session_key=session_key)
   100	
   101	
   102	@dataclass
   103	class OfflineGatewayAdapter:
   104	    def status(self) -> GatewayStatus:
   105	        return GatewayStatus(available=False, detail="gateway offline")
   106	
   107	    def send(self, session_key: str | None, entity_id: str, text: str) -> ChatSendResult:
   108	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.gateway_offline, "gateway offline")

/bin/zsh -lc 'nl -ba src/planner/tickets/logic/fields_codec.py' in /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""The tickets.fields (de)serializer and slot accessors. Pure: json + contracts.
     2	The JSON shape mirrors the DDL default — four field keys, each a slot of
     3	{value, proposal, notes}, proposal being {body, proposed_by, created_at} or null.
     4	with_slot is copy-on-write so decision functions never mutate their input."""
     5	
     6	from __future__ import annotations
     7	
     8	import json
     9	from typing import Any
    10	
    11	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    12	from planner.tickets.contracts import FieldName, FieldSlot, Proposal, TicketFields
    13	
    14	
    15	def _slot_to_dict(slot: FieldSlot) -> dict[str, Any]:
    16	    proposal: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    17	    if slot.proposal is not None:
    18	        proposal = {
    19	            "body": slot.proposal.body,
    20	            "proposed_by": slot.proposal.proposed_by,
    21	            "created_at": slot.proposal.created_at,
    22	        }
    23	    return {"value": slot.value, "proposal": proposal, "notes": slot.notes}
    24	
    25	
    26	def fields_to_json(fields: TicketFields) -> str:
    27	    payload = {
    28	        "success": _slot_to_dict(fields.success),
    29	        "approach": _slot_to_dict(fields.approach),
    30	        "plan": _slot_to_dict(fields.plan),
    31	        "result": _slot_to_dict(fields.result),
    32	    }
    33	    return json.dumps(payload)
    34	
    35	
    36	def _require(condition: bool) -> None:
    37	    if not condition:
    38	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "corrupt ticket fields JSON")
    39	
    40	
    41	def _proposal_from_obj(obj: Any) -> Proposal | None:
    42	    if obj is None:
    43	        return None
    44	    _require(isinstance(obj, dict))
    45	    body = obj.get("body")
    46	    proposed_by = obj.get("proposed_by")
    47	    created_at = obj.get("created_at")
    48	    _require(isinstance(body, str))
    49	    _require(isinstance(proposed_by, str))
    50	    _require(isinstance(created_at, int) and not isinstance(created_at, bool))
    51	    return Proposal(body=body, proposed_by=proposed_by, created_at=created_at)
    52	
    53	
    54	def _slot_from_obj(obj: Any) -> FieldSlot:
    55	    _require(isinstance(obj, dict))
    56	    value = obj.get("value")
    57	    notes = obj.get("notes")
    58	    _require(value is None or isinstance(value, str))
    59	    _require(notes is None or isinstance(notes, str))
    60	    return FieldSlot(value=value, proposal=_proposal_from_obj(obj.get("proposal")), notes=notes)
    61	
    62	
    63	def fields_from_json(raw: str) -> TicketFields:
    64	    data: Any = json.loads(raw)
    65	    _require(isinstance(data, dict))
    66	    for key in ("success", "approach", "plan", "result"):
    67	        _require(key in data)
    68	    return TicketFields(
    69	        success=_slot_from_obj(data["success"]),
    70	        approach=_slot_from_obj(data["approach"]),
    71	        plan=_slot_from_obj(data["plan"]),
    72	        result=_slot_from_obj(data["result"]),
    73	    )
    74	
    75	
    76	def get_slot(fields: TicketFields, field: FieldName) -> FieldSlot:
    77	    if field is FieldName.success:
    78	        return fields.success
    79	    if field is FieldName.approach:
    80	        return fields.approach
    81	    if field is FieldName.plan:
    82	        return fields.plan
    83	    return fields.result
    84	
    85	
    86	def with_slot(fields: TicketFields, field: FieldName, slot: FieldSlot) -> TicketFields:
    87	    return TicketFields(
    88	        success=slot if field is FieldName.success else fields.success,
    89	        approach=slot if field is FieldName.approach else fields.approach,
    90	        plan=slot if field is FieldName.plan else fields.plan,
    91	        result=slot if field is FieldName.result else fields.result,
    92	    )

 succeeded in 0ms:
   181	- `plan queue approvals|pickup|overdue` — the derived views (read-only).
   182	
   183	## 9. HTTP API and events
   184	
   185	- REST JSON under `/api/`: CRUD + actions mirroring Section 8 verbs, PLUS the human actions that deliberately have no CLI form — accept/edit-accept with the grant pair, `needs_review` approve, unblock, grant changes, sprint create/freeze, day-plan accept/accept-all/invalidate/reject-all, drop — and `GET /api/board`, `GET /api/day/<date>`, `GET /api/sprint/current`, `GET /api/queues`. Every mutating endpoint routes through the same canonical functions (one writer per edge).
   186	- `WS /api/events?since=<id>`: tails the `events` table (poll interval 300ms server-side), pushing `{events:[...], cursor}` batches. Used by the UI as an **invalidation signal only** — the UI refetches JSON endpoints (debounced 250ms); it never reconciles event payloads into local state.
   187	- Test mode (Section 13): `POST /api/test/tick-boundary` and `POST /api/test/tick-dispatcher` run one tick synchronously; available only when test mode is on, else 404.
   188	
   189	## 10. The UI
   190	
   191	Six screens, served at `/`. Simplicity is the ruling aesthetic: fewer things done cleanly. Every screen renders from server JSON; refresh at any moment restores identical state.
   192	
   193	Design system (per PRINCIPLES.md, binding):
   194	- One token file, `assets/tokens.css`, owns everything themable: surfaces, text tokens, accent (bright/surface/text), radius scale, motion durations (fast/base/slow), spacing scale, border widths. The app's entire personality must be tunable by editing this one file. No new token categories without evidence of need.
   195	- Type: strict scale, five sizes maximum. Motion: entrances ease out, nothing bounces, all durations from motion tokens. Add nothing to a screen unless it makes the user feel something or a smart person genuinely needs it to understand the screen; no explanatory text for the obvious.
   196	- Before writing any component, record the component inventory for all five screens in decisions.md; build few, reuse hard, no near-duplicates.
   197	
   198	1. **Day (home)**: the brief (rendered markdown); the day-plan tree with per-node Accept / Invalidate and top-level Accept-all / Reject-all; the day's ticket list (ordered); a Review entry showing the pending count; and the day chat panel (Section 11) — quick capture happens here, by telling the planner, which files tickets/ideas via the CLI (Section 17).
   199	2. **Review**: the one-at-a-time review surface — one home of approvals (the Ticket screen is the other), and where the morning pass happens. Pending items — gating-field proposals, sprint-item status proposals, and `needs_review` results — presented one at a time, oldest first. Each shows the proposal rendered, a quick-edit textarea, Accept (with the mandatory grant-pair picker of Section 4.4: how far, and stop-vs-propose — plain pickers, including "no further"), Skip (item stays queued, move on), and a link to the full Ticket. Resolved items leave the queue immediately.
   200	3. **Board**: one column per ticket state (`dropped` hidden), cards show title, priority, deadline, project, pending-proposal marker, running-claim marker. No drag-and-drop in v1: card click opens Ticket.
   201	4. **Ticket**: all four fields as sections (value rendered as markdown; pending proposal shown with Accept/Edit, accepts carrying the onward grant; notes editable inline), recap, state control, ceiling and at-cap as plain pickers (no dial widgetry), a **Copy ticket** button (copies a plain-text block — title, state, priority, field values, recap, links — to the clipboard for pasting into any agent or editor), links, day/sprint assignment, run history, event log, and the chat panel (Section 11).
   202	5. **Sprint**: kickoff fields, items grouped by status (with ticket rollup counts and blockers-cleared flags), loose tickets, review fields (frozen states shown as locked).
   203	6. **Backlog & Ideas**: itemless sprint items list (by priority) and ideas list, each with create forms.
   204

   402	### 3.3 `skills/planner-main.md` — the day/system chat agent (~90 lines)
   403	
   404	1. **You** — the planner in the day chat. You read and file through the CLI
   405	   (`PLAN_SERVER_URL` set; you carry no claim); all decisions (accepts, grants,
   406	   approvals, day-plan calls) belong to the human in the UI — you surface, summarize,
   407	   and capture, you never resolve.
   408	2. **Quick capture — the prime directive** — anything the human tosses into chat that
   409	   is work gets filed IMMEDIATELY as a ticket, anything that is an idea as an idea;
   410	   default P3; capture first, keep talking after:
   411	   `plan ticket create --title "…"` (add `--project`/`--deadline` only when stated);
   412	   `plan idea create --title "…" --body-file -` for bodies. Never ask "should I file
   413	   this?" — file it and say so.
   414	3. **Operating the queues** — `plan queue approvals --json` (what waits on the human,
   415	   oldest first), `plan queue pickup --json` (what the dispatcher can take next, in
   416	   order), `plan queue overdue --json` (past-deadline tickets/items). Transcribed
   417	   one-line output examples for each.
   418	4. **The day** — `plan day show --json` (brief, plan tree, ordered ticket list);
   419	   `plan day add-ticket <t_id>` / `plan day remove-ticket <t_id>` (today by default;
   420	   removing defers — ticket state untouched); `plan ticket set <t_id> --day today`.
   421	5. **Looking things up** — `plan ticket show <t_id> --json`, `plan ticket list
   422	   --state …`, `plan item list`, `plan sprint show --json`, `plan idea list`.
   423	6. **What you must route to the human** — accepting proposals, grants
   424	   (ceiling/at_cap), needs_review approval, unblocking, dropping, day-plan
   425	   accept/invalidate: name the thing, point to the Review screen or Ticket page.

1. The 12-step table omits the item-35 day-plan decision.
Evidence: [SPEC.md:314](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/SPEC.md:314) requires human “day-plan decisions” through HTTP API. The plan’s only day step is CLI day add/remove at [plan.md:267](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T21-dogfood-skills/plan.md:267), and there is no `/api/day/{date}/plan/...` step. The actual HTTP-only day-plan routes are [days/api.py:150](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/src/planner/days/api.py:150), [days/api.py:166](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/src/planner/days/api.py:166), [days/api.py:178](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/src/planner/days/api.py:178), [days/api.py:198](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/src/planner/days/api.py:198).
What the plan should say instead: add a header-less HTTP-human day-plan decision, e.g. accept a node on the demo day via `POST /api/day/<demo-real-today>/plan/accept {"node": 1}` and assert the returned day plan status.

2. The plan pins all ticket reads as `ticket_json`, but `ticket show` / `GET /api/tickets/{id}` returns the detail shape.
Evidence: [plan.md:201](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T21-dogfood-skills/plan.md:201) says every ticket mutation/read is `ticket_json` unless noted. `ticket show` calls `GET /api/tickets/{tid}` at [cli/main.py:275](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/src/planner/cli/main.py:275), that route returns `ticket_detail` at [tickets/api.py:258](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/src/planner/tickets/api.py:258), and `ticket_detail` adds `blocked`, `links`, `day_ids`, and `run_summary` at [tickets/views.py:121](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/src/planner/tickets/views.py:121).
What the plan should say instead: mutations return `ticket_json`; `ticket show` / `GET /api/tickets/{id}` returns `ticket_detail` (`ticket_json` plus detail-only fields).

3. The deliverables exclude the required `DOCS.md` skills summary.
Evidence: [plan.md:3](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T21-dogfood-skills/plan.md:3) says only the script, e2e test, and four skills are created/touched. SPEC §17 requires the four skills and says “DOCS.md summarizes their roles” at [SPEC.md:243](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/SPEC.md:243); required artifacts also say DOCS must explain “what the skills are for” at [SPEC.md:257](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/SPEC.md:257).
What the plan should say instead: include a scoped `DOCS.md` update summarizing the four skill roles, or explicitly assign that required update elsewhere and remove “nothing else is touched.”

4. `planning-worker.md` misstates the CLI exit-code surface.
Evidence: [plan.md:371](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T21-dogfood-skills/plan.md:371) says exit code 1 means “rejected write.” SPEC says exit code 1 is “validation/domain error” and 2 is “connection error” at [SPEC.md:171](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/SPEC.md:171). The CLI implements `EXIT_ERROR = 1` and uses it for client validation and any failed HTTP response at [cli/http.py:19](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/src/planner/cli/http.py:19), [cli/http.py:83](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/src/planner/cli/http.py:83), [cli/http.py:104](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/src/planner/cli/http.py:104).
What the plan should say instead: `0` success, `1` validation/domain error, `2` connection/transport error; rejected writes are only one kind of exit-1 failure.

5. `planner-main.md` routes day-plan decisions to the wrong UI surface.
Evidence: [plan.md:423](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T21-dogfood-skills/plan.md:423) says day-plan accept/invalidate should point to the Review screen or Ticket page. SPEC puts the day-plan tree and Accept/Invalidate/Accept-all/Reject-all controls on Day/home at [SPEC.md:198](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/SPEC.md:198); Review and Ticket cover approvals/ticket fields at [SPEC.md:199](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/SPEC.md:199) and [SPEC.md:201](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/SPEC.md:201).
What the plan should say instead: route day-plan accept/invalidate/reject-all/accept-all to the Day screen; route proposal accepts and needs_review approval to Review/Ticket.
